# List 28

## Unit 1

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>abreast</b></td><td>/əˈbrest/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>副词、形容词</td><td>熟知的</td><td>keep abreast of the news<br>及时了解新闻</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She reads three newspapers daily to keep abreast of political developments.  她每天读三份报纸，以便及时了解政治动态。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 acquainted</td><td>/əˈkweɪntɪd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>熟悉的，认识的</td><td>be acquainted with<br>熟悉；与……相识</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 I am not well acquainted with the rules of this game.  我对这个游戏的规则不太熟悉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 conversant</td><td>/kənˈvɜːrsnt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>熟悉的，通晓的</td><td>conversant with the law<br>通晓法律</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She is fully conversant with the new safety regulations.  她非常熟悉新的安全条例。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 informed</td><td>/ɪnˈfɔːrmd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>见识广的，掌握信息的</td><td>make an informed decision<br>做出明智的决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Voters need accurate data to make an informed choice.  选民需要准确的数据才能做出明智的选择。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 knowledgeable</td><td>/ˈnɑːlədʒəbəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>知识丰富的，了解很多的</td><td>knowledgeable about wine<br>懂葡萄酒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Our guide was remarkably knowledgeable about local birds.  我们的导游对当地鸟类了解得非常多。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 versed</td><td>/vɜːrst/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>熟知的；精通的</td><td>well versed in history<br>精通历史</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He is well versed in both classical and modern poetry.  他对古典诗歌和现代诗歌都很精通。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 well-informed</td><td>/ˌwel ɪnˈfɔrmd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>见闻广博的，消息灵通的</td><td>a well-informed source<br>消息可靠的来源</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A well-informed reader spotted the error in the report.  一位见闻广博的读者发现了报告中的错误。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ignorant</td><td>/ˈɪɡnərənt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无知的，不知道的</td><td>ignorant of the facts<br>不了解事实</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They were completely ignorant of the danger ahead.  他们对前方的危险毫不知情。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unacquainted</td><td>/ˌʌnəˈkweɪntɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不熟悉的，不了解的</td><td>unacquainted with the area<br>不熟悉这个地区</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Being unacquainted with the city, he got lost very quickly.  由于不熟悉这座城市，他很快就迷路了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unfamiliar</td><td>/ˌʌnfəˈmɪljər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不熟悉的，陌生的</td><td>an unfamiliar face<br>一张陌生的脸</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The street names here were all unfamiliar to me.  这里的街名对我来说都很陌生。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 uninformed</td><td>/ˌʌnɪnˈfɔːrmd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不了解情况的，无知的</td><td>an uninformed opinion<br>不明情况的看法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Uninformed criticism rarely helps anyone improve their work.  不了解情况的批评很少能帮助别人改进工作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 unknowledgeable</del></td><td>/ˌʌnˈnɑlɪdʒəbəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>缺乏知识的，不懂行的</td><td>unknowledgeable about finance<br>不懂金融</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He felt unknowledgeable about finance and hired an adviser.  他觉得自己不懂金融，于是聘请了一位顾问。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del><b>appurtenance</b></del></td><td>/əˈpɜːrtənəns/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>附属物，附属权益</td><td>the appurtenances of power<br>权力的附属物</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He clearly enjoyed all the appurtenances of high office.  他显然享受着高位带来的一切附属特权。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 accessory</td><td>/əkˈsesəri/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>辅助的，附属的</td><td>an accessory building<br>一座附属建筑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The garage is an accessory structure behind the main house.  车库是主屋后面的一座附属建筑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 adjunct</td><td>/ˈædʒʌŋkt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>附属物，非必须部分</td><td>a useful adjunct to therapy<br>治疗的有益辅助手段</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Exercise is a valuable adjunct to a healthy diet.  运动是健康饮食的有益辅助。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 affiliate</td><td>/əˈfɪliˌeɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>使隶属，加入；分支机构</td><td>affiliate with a university<br>隶属于某大学</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The clinic chose to affiliate with a large teaching hospital.  这家诊所选择隶属于一所大型教学医院。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 appendage</td><td>/əˈpendɪdʒ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>附属物，附加物</td><td>a mere appendage<br>仅仅是个附属物</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The small museum is treated as an appendage of the university.  这座小博物馆被当作大学的附属机构。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 appendix</td><td>/əˈpendɪks/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>附录；阑尾</td><td>in the appendix<br>在附录中</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The full data set appears in the appendix at the back.  完整数据见书后附录。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 attachment</td><td>/əˈtætʃmənt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>附件，附加装置；依恋</td><td>a vacuum cleaner attachment<br>吸尘器配件</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The drill comes with an attachment for sanding wood.  这台电钻附带一个打磨木头的配件。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>acclaim</b></td><td>/əˈkleɪm/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>称赞</td><td>win critical acclaim<br>赢得评论界赞誉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her first novel won wide critical acclaim across Europe and Asia.  她的第一部小说在欧洲和亚洲赢得了广泛的评论界赞誉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 accolade</td><td>/ˈækəleɪd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>赞扬，荣誉；奖励</td><td>win the highest accolade<br>赢得最高荣誉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The novel received accolades from critics on both continents.  这部小说获得了两大洲评论家的赞誉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 applause</td><td>/əˈplɔːz/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>鼓掌；认可，赞许</td><td>a round of applause<br>一阵掌声</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her speech was greeted with long and warm applause.  她的演讲赢得了长时间热烈的掌声。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 credit</td><td>/ˈkredɪt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>表扬，赞扬；信任</td><td>take credit for<br>把功劳归于自己</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She deserves credit for keeping the project alive.  她值得称赞，因为她让这个项目坚持下来了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 distinction</td><td>/dɪˈstɪŋkʃən/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>殊荣，卓越；区别</td><td>a mark of distinction<br>卓越的标志</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He graduated with distinction from a small rural college.  他以优异成绩从一所乡下的小型学院毕业。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 homage</td><td>/ˈhɑːmɪdʒ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>尊敬，敬意</td><td>pay homage to<br>向…致敬</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Thousands came to pay homage to the poet on her ninetieth birthday.  数千人在诗人九十岁生日那天前来致敬。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 honor</td><td>/ˈɑːnər/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>尊敬，给以荣誉；荣誉</td><td>honor the veterans<br>表彰老兵</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The city honored the veterans with a quiet morning ceremony.  该市用一场安静的晨间仪式表彰老兵。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 kudos</td><td>/ˈkuːdoʊz/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>名望，名声；夸奖，赞扬</td><td>kudos for the win<br>因获胜而受赞扬</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She earned kudos from critics for her fearless debut novel.  她凭无畏的处女作小说赢得了评论界的赞誉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 laud</td><td>/ˈlɔːd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>赞美，称赞</td><td>laud his courage<br>赞扬他的勇气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics lauded the young pianist for her fearless interpretation.  评论家赞扬这位年轻钢琴家大胆的演绎。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 laurels</td><td>/ˈlɔːrəlz/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>荣誉，殊荣</td><td>rest on one's laurels<br>满足于既有荣誉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After one prize he rested on his laurels for a decade.  拿过一个奖后他十年都满足于既有荣誉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 accredit</td><td>/əˈkredɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>认可，授权；归功于</td><td>accredit the university<br>认可该大学资质</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The board finally accredited the new nursing program.  委员会终于认可了这个新的护理专业。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 applaud</td><td>/əˈplɔːd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>称赞，赞成；鼓掌</td><td>applaud the decision<br>赞成这一决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Teachers applauded the decision to reduce homework for young pupils.  老师们赞成给低年级学生减少作业的决定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cheer</td><td>/ˈtʃɪr/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>欢呼，喝彩；使高兴</td><td>a loud cheer<br>一阵响亮的欢呼</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A loud cheer rose from the crowd when the goal was scored.  进球时人群中响起一阵响亮的欢呼。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 commend</td><td>/kəˈmend/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>赞扬，表扬</td><td>commend her courage<br>赞扬她的勇气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The principal commended her courage in front of the whole school.  校长在全校面前赞扬了她的勇气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 endorse</td><td>/ɪnˈdɔːrs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>公开支持</td><td>endorse a candidate<br>公开支持某候选人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The union voted to endorse the mayor for a second term.  工会投票支持市长连任。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 exalt</td><td>/ɪɡˈzɔːlt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>赞扬；提升（地位）</td><td>exalt to the skies<br>推崇到极点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics exalt her early novels as masterpieces of the era.  评论家将她早期的小说赞为那个时代的杰作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hail</td><td>/ˈheɪl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>冰雹；一阵（如子弹）</td><td>a hail of criticism<br>一阵猛烈的批评</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The soldiers advanced through a hail of bullets and smoke.  士兵们在弹雨与烟尘中前进。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 praise</td><td>/ˈpreɪz/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>赞扬，称赞</td><td>win high praise<br>获得高度赞扬</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her first novel won high praise from serious critics.  她的第一部小说赢得了严肃评论家的高度赞扬。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 salute</td><td>/səˈluːt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>致敬，赞扬；敬礼</td><td>salute their courage<br>向他们的勇气致敬</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The mayor saluted the firefighters for their courage that night.  市长向消防员那晚的勇气致敬。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tout</td><td>/taʊt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>极力赞扬</td><td>tout a new product<br>极力吹捧新产品</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Advertisements tout the new drug as a cure for nearly every ailment.  广告把这种新药吹捧成几乎万能的良药。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 castigate</td><td>/ˈkæstɪɡeɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>强烈（公开）指责</td><td>castigate for negligence<br>因疏忽而受斥责</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge castigated the company for its negligence.  法官严厉指责该公司的疏忽。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 excoriate</td><td>/ɪkˈskɔːrieɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>严厉批评；擦伤皮肤</td><td>excoriate the government<br>严厉批评政府</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics excoriated the film for its casual cruelty.  批评家严厉批评该片的随意残忍。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 lambaste</td><td>/læmˈbeɪst/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>严厉斥责，痛骂</td><td>lambaste the government<br>痛斥政府</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics lambasted the film for its shallow, sentimental ending.  批评家严厉斥责这部电影结局浅薄而煽情。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 pan</td><td>/pæn/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>严厉批评</td><td>pan a new film<br>严厉批评一部新片</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics panned the film for its clumsy dialogue and predictable ending.  影评人严厉批评这部电影对话笨拙、结局可预料。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 slam</td><td>/ˈslæm/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>严厉批评；猛击</td><td>slam the proposal<br>猛烈批评该提案</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics slammed the film for its careless plotting.  评论家因情节草率而猛烈批评这部电影。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>apostle</b></td><td>/əˈpɑːsl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>（政策或思想等的）信奉者，支持者</td><td>an apostle of reform<br>改革的倡导者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He became a tireless apostle of reform in the state legislature.  他成了州议会中不懈的改革倡导者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 advocate</td><td>/ˈædvəkeɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>支持，提倡</td><td>advocate reform<br>提倡改革</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Many doctors advocate walking as the simplest form of exercise.  许多医生提倡把走路作为最简单的运动方式。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 champion</td><td>/ˈtʃæmpiən/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>支持</td><td>champion a cause<br>支持一项事业</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She has championed the rights of migrant workers for years.  她多年来一直为农民工的权益奔走呼吁。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 crusader</td><td>/kruːˈseɪdər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>（改革运动的）倡导者，斗士</td><td>a tireless crusader<br>不懈的倡导者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She became a tireless crusader for clean drinking water.  她成了不懈倡导清洁饮水的斗士。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 expounder</del></td><td>/ɪkˈspaʊndər/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>阐述者，讲解者</td><td>an expounder of doctrine<br>教义的阐述者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He became the movement's clearest expounder and busiest public speaker.  他成了这场运动最清晰的阐述者和最忙的演讲人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 espouser</del></td><td>/ɪˈspaʊzər/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>支持者，拥护者</td><td>an espouser of the cause<br>该事业的拥护者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 As an early espouser of the theory, he faced constant ridicule.  作为该理论的早期拥护者，他不断受到嘲笑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 exponent</td><td>/ɪkˈspoʊnənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>倡导者，代表人物；指数</td><td>a leading exponent<br>主要的倡导者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He became a leading exponent of modern jazz in the fifties.  他在五十年代成为现代爵士乐的主要倡导者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 partisan</td><td>/ˈpɑːrtəzn/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>党派偏见的；盲目拥护者</td><td>partisan politics<br>党派偏见的政治</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The debate quickly sank into partisan shouting about very old grievances.  辩论很快沦为带党派偏见的叫嚷，纠缠陈年旧怨。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 promoter</td><td>/prəˈmoʊtər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>支持者，倡导者；主办人</td><td>a promoter of literacy<br>识字运动的倡导者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The concert promoter guaranteed the band a fee regardless of ticket sales.  演出主办方无论票房如何都保证付给乐队报酬。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 proponent</td><td>/prəˈpoʊnənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>建议者，支持者</td><td>a proponent of change<br>变革的支持者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She became the leading proponent of change within the old institution.  她成了这家老机构内部倡导变革的主要支持者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 protagonist</td><td>/proʊˈtæɡənəst/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>倡导者，支持者；主角</td><td>a leading protagonist<br>主要倡导者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She was a leading protagonist of free public transport in the city.  她是本市免费公交的主要倡导者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 supporter</td><td>/səˈpɔːrtər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>支持者，拥护者</td><td>a loyal supporter<br>忠诚的支持者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Thousands of supporters gathered outside the stadium before kickoff.  开赛前数千名支持者聚集在体育场外。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 zealot</td><td>/ˈzelət/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>狂热者，极端主义者</td><td>a religious zealot<br>宗教狂热者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Only a zealot would defend that policy so fiercely.  只有狂热者才会如此激烈地为那项政策辩护。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 adversary</td><td>/ˈædvərseri/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>敌手，对手</td><td>a formidable adversary<br>强劲的对手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He respected his adversary even while fighting to win.  即使在拼命求胜时，他也尊重自己的对手。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 antagonist</td><td>/ænˈtæɡənɪst/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>对手，敌人</td><td>a bitter antagonist<br>势不两立的对手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her chief antagonist in the debate was a former professor.  她在辩论中的主要对手是一位前教授。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 opponent</td><td>/əˈpoʊnənt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>对手，反对者</td><td>a worthy opponent<br>值得尊敬的对手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She defeated a much stronger opponent in the final round.  她在决赛中击败了一位强得多的对手。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>accord</b></td><td>/əˈkɔːrd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>一致，符合；授予</td><td>in accord with<br>与…一致</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His actions were in accord with the policy he announced.  他的行动与他宣布的政策一致。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 accordance</td><td>/əˈkɔːrdəns/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>一致，符合</td><td>in accordance with rules<br>依照规定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 All payments were made in accordance with the signed contract.  所有付款均依照已签合同进行。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 agreement</td><td>/əˈɡriːmənt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>一致，同意；协议</td><td>come to an agreement<br>达成协议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two firms signed an agreement to share research costs.  两家公司签署协议分担研究费用。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 conformity</td><td>/kənˈfɔːrməti/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>遵从，一致，顺从</td><td>conformity to social norms<br>遵从社会规范</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company demands strict conformity to its dress code.  公司要求严格遵守其着装规范。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 congruence</td><td>/ˈkɑːŋɡruəns/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>一致，契合</td><td>congruence between words and deeds<br>言行一致</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 There was little congruence between his words and his actions.  他的言语和行为几乎不一致。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 congruity</td><td>/kənˈɡruːəti/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>一致性，协调</td><td>a pleasing congruity<br>令人愉悦的协调</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The garden showed a pleasing congruity of color and form.  这座花园显示出色彩与形式的协调。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 consensus</td><td>/kənˈsensəs/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>一致同意</td><td>reach a consensus<br>达成一致意见</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The board finally reached a consensus after four hours of debate.  董事会经过四小时辩论终于达成了一致。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 consonance</td><td>/ˈkɑːnsənəns/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>一致，和谐</td><td>in consonance with<br>与…相符一致</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her behavior was in consonance with her stated beliefs.  她的行为与她宣称的信念一致。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 harmony</td><td>/ˈhɑːrməni/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>和谐，融洽</td><td>live in harmony with<br>与…和谐共处</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two communities have lived in harmony for generations.  这两个社区世代和睦相处。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tune</td><td>/ˈtuːn/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>调子；使协调，调音</td><td>in tune with<br>与…协调一致</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His proposals are in tune with what most voters want.  他的提案与大多数选民的意愿一致。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 agree</td><td>/əˈɡriː/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>同意，赞同；一致</td><td>agree to the terms<br>同意这些条款</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Both sides finally agreed to the new safety rules.  双方最终同意了新的安全规定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 chord</td><td>/kɔːrd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>与…和谐一致；符合</td><td>chord with his beliefs<br>与他的信念相符</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His plain proposal chords with everything he has said before.  他直白的提议与他此前所说的完全一致。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cohere</td><td>/koʊˈhɪr/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>一致，相符；黏合</td><td>cohere with the evidence<br>与证据相符</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His account does not cohere with what the other witnesses said.  他的说法与其他证人所言并不相符。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 coincide</td><td>/ˌkoʊɪnˈsaɪd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>同时发生；相符，一致</td><td>coincide with the holiday<br>与假日同时发生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The festival coincides with the first full moon of autumn.  这个节日与秋天第一次满月同时到来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 conform</td><td>/kənˈfɔːrm/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>使协调；遵照，遵从</td><td>conform to the standard<br>符合标准</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 All new buildings must conform to strict safety codes.  所有新建筑都必须符合严格的安全规范。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 correspond</td><td>/ˌkɔːrəˈspɑːnd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>相符，一致；通信</td><td>correspond to the facts<br>与事实相符</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The figures in the report correspond exactly to the bank records.  报告中的数字与银行记录完全相符。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fit</td><td>/ˈfɪt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、形容词</td><td>相符，适合；合适的</td><td>fit the description<br>符合描述</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The suspect they arrested did not fit the witness's description.  他们逮捕的嫌疑人并不符合证人的描述。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 harmonize</td><td>/ˈhɑːrməˌnaɪz/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>协调一致；使和谐</td><td>harmonize with tradition<br>与传统相协调</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new building harmonizes well with the older houses nearby.  这座新建筑与附近的老房子十分协调。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 jibe</td><td>/dʒaɪb/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>意见一致</td><td>jibe with the facts<br>与事实相符</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His account of the accident does not jibe with the physical evidence.  他对这场事故的说法与实物证据不符。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sort</td><td>/ˈsɔːrt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>相符，一致；分类</td><td>sort with his character<br>与他的性格相符</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Such rudeness does not sort with her usual kindness.  这种无礼与她平日的善良并不相符。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 square</td><td>/ˈskwer/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>贿赂，收买；使一致</td><td>square the local officials<br>把当地官员收买好</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The developer had squared the local officials long before construction.  开发商早在动工前就把当地官员收买好了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tally</td><td>/ˈtæli/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>相符，一致；计数</td><td>tally with the records<br>与记录相符</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His account of the accident does not tally with the witness statements.  他对事故的叙述与证人陈述不符。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 award</td><td>/əˈwɔːrd/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>授予，颁发；奖励</td><td>award a medal<br>颁发奖章</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The committee awarded her a medal for outstanding service.  委员会因她的杰出贡献授予她一枚奖章。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 grant</td><td>/ˈɡrænt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>承认；授予，同意给予</td><td>grant that he is right<br>承认他是对的</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 I grant that the plan has some obvious weaknesses.  我承认这个计划有些明显的弱点。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 vest</td><td>/ˈvest/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>授予（权力）；背心</td><td>vest authority in<br>将权力授予</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The constitution vests final authority in the highest court.  宪法将最终权力授予最高法院。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 vouchsafe</td><td>/ˌvaʊtʃˈseɪf/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>允诺，给予</td><td>vouchsafe an answer<br>赐予一个答复</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The minister finally vouchsafed an answer to the waiting reporters.  部长终于给等候的记者一个答复。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 conflict</td><td>/ˈkɑːnflɪkt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>冲突，矛盾；抵触</td><td>a conflict of interest<br>利益冲突</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge withdrew because of a possible conflict of interest.  法官因可能存在利益冲突而回避。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 disagreement</td><td>/dɪsəˈɡriːmənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>分歧，不一致，争执</td><td>a sharp disagreement<br>激烈的分歧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A long disagreement over funding stalled the whole project.  关于资金的长期分歧使整个项目停滞。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 incongruence</td><td>/ˌɪnkənˈɡruːəns/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>不一致，不协调</td><td>incongruence between goals<br>目标之间的不一致</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The report noted a clear incongruence between policy and practice.  报告指出政策与实践之间明显不一致。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 incongruity</td><td>/ˌɪnkənˈɡruːəti/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>不协调，不一致</td><td>a glaring incongruity<br>明显的不协调</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The incongruity between his words and his actions puzzled everyone.  他言行之间的不一致让所有人困惑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 incongruousness</del></td><td>/ɪnˈkɑːŋɡruəsnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>不协调，不相称</td><td>the incongruousness of the pairing<br>这种搭配的不协调</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Everyone smiled at the incongruousness of his formal suit and sandals.  他正装配凉鞋的不协调让所有人发笑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 withhold</td><td>/wɪðˈhoʊld/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>抑制；扣压，不给予</td><td>withhold information<br>隐瞒信息</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was accused of withholding key information.  他被指控隐瞒关键信息。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>accustom</b></td><td>/əˈkʌstəm/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使习惯</td><td>accustom oneself to cold<br>使自己适应寒冷</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He had to accustom himself to the cold Boston winters very quickly.  他必须很快让自己习惯波士顿寒冷的冬天。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 familiarize</td><td>/fəˈmɪliəraɪz/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使熟悉，使了解</td><td>familiarize yourself with the rules<br>让自己熟悉规则</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 New staff must familiarize themselves with safety procedures.  新员工必须熟悉安全流程。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 habituate</td><td>/həˈbɪtʃuˌeɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使习惯于</td><td>habituate oneself to cold<br>使自己习惯寒冷</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Divers slowly habituate themselves to the pressure of deep water.  潜水员慢慢让自己习惯深水的压力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 inure</td><td>/ɪˈnjʊr/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使习惯接受不好的东西</td><td>inured to hardship<br>习惯了艰苦</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Years at sea had inured the fishermen to cold, wet, and hunger.  多年的海上生活使渔民们习惯了寒冷、潮湿和饥饿。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 orient</td><td>/ˈɔːriˌent/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使确定方向，使熟悉或适应</td><td>orient oneself to the city<br>熟悉这座城市</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 New employees need a week to orient themselves to the company culture.  新员工需要一周时间来熟悉公司文化。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 wont</td><td>/ˈwoʊnt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>习惯的；习惯</td><td>as is his wont<br>一如他的习惯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He rose at five, as was his wont in the summer.  他五点起床，这是他夏天的习惯。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 alienate</td><td>/ˈeɪliəneɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>疏远，离间；使疏离</td><td>alienate one's supporters<br>疏远自己的支持者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His harsh remarks alienated many longtime friends within a single evening.  他刻薄的言辞在一个晚上就疏远了许多老朋友。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 estrange</td><td>/ɪˈstreɪndʒ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使疏远，离间，使感情失和</td><td>estrange the two brothers<br>离间两兄弟</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A long quarrel over money estranged the two brothers for years.  一场长久的金钱纠纷使两兄弟多年感情失和。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 wean</td><td>/ˈwiːn/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使断奶；使戒除</td><td>wean him off sugar<br>让他戒掉糖</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The clinic gradually weaned him off sugary drinks.  诊所逐步让他戒掉了含糖饮料。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>acquire</b></td><td>/əˈkwaɪər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>获得；取得；逐渐养成</td><td>acquire a taste for<br>逐渐喜欢上</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It took years for him to acquire a taste for olives.  他花了好几年才慢慢喜欢上橄榄。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 attain</td><td>/əˈteɪn/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>达到，获得</td><td>attain a goal<br>达成目标</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She attained her doctorate before turning twenty-six.  她在二十六岁前取得了博士学位。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 capture</td><td>/ˈkæptʃər/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>夺得，攫取；捕获</td><td>capture market share<br>夺取市场份额</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The startup captured a large share of the market within a year.  这家初创公司一年内夺取了很大市场份额。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 draw</td><td>/ˈdrɔː/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>取得，获得；拉，画</td><td>draw a conclusion<br>得出结论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We drew useful lessons from the failed experiment.  我们从失败的实验中获得了有用的教训。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 gain</td><td>/ˈɡeɪn/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>获得，赢得</td><td>gain recognition<br>获得认可</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her essays gained recognition well beyond the campus.  她的文章赢得了远超校园的认可。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 garner</td><td>/ˈɡɑːrnər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>获得，积累</td><td>garner support<br>获得支持</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The proposal garnered support from both major parties.  该提案获得了两大党的支持。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 get</td><td>/ˈɡet/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>患上（疾病）；获得</td><td>get the flu<br>得流感</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Half the office got the flu during that freezing week.  那个严寒的星期里办公室一半人得了流感。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 make</td><td>/ˈmeɪk/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>制造，做成；使成为</td><td>make a wooden chair<br>做一把木椅</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 My grandfather could make a wooden chair with only simple tools.  我祖父只用简单工具就能做出一把木椅。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 obtain</td><td>/əbˈteɪn/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>获得，得到</td><td>obtain a permit<br>取得许可证</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 You must obtain a permit before cutting down any trees.  砍伐任何树木前你必须取得许可证。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 procure</td><td>/prəˈkjʊr/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>获得，取得</td><td>procure weapons illegally<br>非法获取武器</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The smugglers tried to procure weapons through a network of middlemen.  走私者试图通过中间人网络获取武器。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 realize</td><td>/ˈriːəˌlaɪz/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>获得（利润）；实现；意识到</td><td>realize a profit<br>获得利润</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They realized a modest profit on the sale of the land.  他们在出售那块地时获得了不多的利润。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 reap</td><td>/ˈriːp/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>收获，获得</td><td>reap the benefits<br>获得好处</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Patient investors will eventually reap the benefits of the reform.  有耐心的投资者最终会获得改革的好处。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 secure</td><td>/sɪˈkjʊr/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词、及物动词</td><td>牢固的，安全的</td><td>a secure connection<br>安全的连接</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Make sure the ladder is secure before you start climbing.  开始攀爬前确保梯子牢固。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 win</td><td>/ˈwɪn/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>赢得，获胜</td><td>win an award<br>获奖</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their small documentary won an award in Berlin.  他们的小纪录片在柏林获奖。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bring in</td><td>/brɪŋ ˈɪn/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>带来（收入）；引进</td><td>bring in extra income<br>带来额外收入</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The weekend market brings in more cash than the whole weekday trade.  周末集市带来的现金比整个工作日的生意还多。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 forfeit</td><td>/ˈfɔːrfət/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>丧失，被没收（权利、财物）</td><td>forfeit the right to appeal<br>丧失上诉权</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He forfeited his right to appeal by missing the deadline.  他因错过期限而丧失了上诉权。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 lose</td><td>/ˈluːz/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>失去；丢失</td><td>lose everything overnight<br>一夜间失去一切</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Careless investors can lose everything overnight when a bubble finally bursts.  泡沫破裂时，粗心的投资者可能一夜之间失去一切。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>acquisitive</b></td><td>/əˈkwɪzətɪv/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>贪婪的</td><td>an acquisitive society<br>物欲横流的社会</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The acquisitive collector bought every painting that came onto the market.  这位贪得无厌的收藏家买下了市面上出现的每一幅画。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 avaricious</td><td>/ˌævəˈrɪʃəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>贪财的，贪婪的</td><td>an avaricious landlord<br>贪财的房东</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The avaricious banker cared for nothing but the size of his bonus.  这个贪财的银行家只在乎奖金的多少。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 avid</td><td>/ˈævɪd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>急切渴望的，热衷的</td><td>an avid reader<br>热衷阅读的人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 My grandmother is an avid gardener even in winter.  我祖母即使在冬天也是个热衷园艺的人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 coveting</td><td>/ˈkʌvɪtɪŋ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>贪求，垂涎</td><td>coveting a neighbor's wealth<br>垂涎邻人的财富</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Endless coveting of other people's houses made him miserable.  不停垂涎别人的房子让他很痛苦。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 covetous</td><td>/ˈkʌvətəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>贪婪的，渴求财富的</td><td>covetous of his wealth<br>贪求他的财富</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He cast a covetous eye on his neighbor's newly bought tractor.  他贪婪地盯着邻居新买的拖拉机。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 grabby</td><td>/ˈɡræbi/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>贪得无厌的</td><td>grabby developers<br>贪婪的开发商</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Locals accused the grabby developers of seizing public land.  当地人指责贪婪的开发商侵占公地。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 grasping</td><td>/ˈɡræspɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>贪婪的，唯利是图的</td><td>a grasping moneylender<br>唯利是图的放贷人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His grasping relatives fought over the estate before the funeral ended.  他贪婪的亲戚在葬礼结束前就争夺遗产。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mercenary</td><td>/ˈmɜːrsəneri/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>唯利是图的，贪婪的</td><td>mercenary motives<br>唯利是图的动机</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her mercenary motives became obvious once the inheritance was finally mentioned.  一提到遗产，她唯利是图的动机就显露无遗。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 moneygrubbing</del></td><td>/ˈmʌniɡrʌbɪŋ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>贪财的，唯利是图的</td><td>a moneygrubbing landlord<br>唯利是图的房东</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Neighbors called him a moneygrubbing landlord behind his back.  邻居们背后叫他唯利是图的房东。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rapacious</td><td>/rəˈpeɪʃəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>过度贪婪的</td><td>rapacious business practices<br>贪婪的商业行径</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Rapacious business practices drove several small competitors out of the market.  贪婪的商业行径把几家小竞争者挤出了市场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 benevolent</td><td>/bəˈnevələnt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>仁慈的，行善的</td><td>a benevolent employer<br>仁慈的雇主</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A benevolent stranger paid for the family's train tickets.  一位仁慈的陌生人为这家人买了火车票。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 generous</td><td>/ˈdʒenərəs/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>丰富的，大量的；慷慨的</td><td>a generous helping<br>满满的一份</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She poured a generous glass of wine for each guest.  她给每位客人倒了满满一杯酒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 liberal</td><td>/ˈlɪbərəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>思想前卫的；慷慨的，大方的</td><td>liberal social views<br>前卫的社会观念</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His liberal social views often surprised the older members of the club.  他前卫的社会观念常常让俱乐部里年长的成员感到意外。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 philanthropic</td><td>/ˌfɪlənˈθrɑːpɪk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>博爱的，为他人着想的</td><td>philanthropic donations<br>慈善捐赠</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Philanthropic donations paid for the new hospital wing for sick children.  慈善捐赠支付了收治病童的医院新翼楼。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 munificent</td><td>/mjuːˈnɪfɪsnt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>慷慨的</td><td>a munificent donation<br>一笔慷慨的捐赠</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The university received a munificent donation from a former engineering student.  这所大学收到了一位工程系校友的慷慨捐赠。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>adjudicate</b></td><td>/əˈdʒuːdɪkeɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>裁决，判定</td><td>adjudicate a dispute<br>裁决一起纠纷</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A retired judge was asked to adjudicate the long-running border dispute.  一位退休法官被请来裁决这场旷日持久的边界纠纷。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 adjudge</td><td>/əˈdʒʌdʒ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>裁定，判决；宣判</td><td>adjudge him guilty<br>判定他有罪</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The tribunal adjudged the contract void from the start.  法庭裁定该合同自始无效。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 arbitrate</td><td>/ˈɑːrbəˌtreɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>仲裁，公断</td><td>arbitrate between the parties<br>在双方之间仲裁</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A retired judge was asked to arbitrate the family's property quarrel.  一位退休法官受邀仲裁这家人的财产纷争。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 decide</td><td>/dɪˈsaɪd/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>裁决，判定；决定</td><td>decide the case<br>对案件作出裁决</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It took the panel two days to decide who deserved the prize.  评审团花了两天才裁定谁该得奖。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 determine</td><td>/dəˈtɜːrmən/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>判定，确定；决心</td><td>determine the cause<br>判定原因</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Scientists could not determine the cause of the sudden outbreak.  科学家无法判定这次突发疫情的原因。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 judge</td><td>/ˈdʒʌdʒ/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>评判者，裁判；法官；判断</td><td>a panel of judges<br>一个评审团</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Three judges scored each performance without conferring with one another.  三名评委各自评分，互不商议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 referee</td><td>/ˌrefəˈriː/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>仲裁者；裁判员</td><td>act as referee<br>担任仲裁人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A respected professor agreed to act as referee in the bitter dispute.  一位受人尊敬的教授同意在这场激烈争端中担任仲裁人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 settle</td><td>/ˈsetəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>解决，平息；定居</td><td>settle the dispute<br>解决争端</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two sides finally settled their dispute out of court.  双方最终在庭外解决了争端。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 umpire</td><td>/ˈʌmˌpaɪər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>裁判员；仲裁人</td><td>the umpire's decision<br>裁判的判决</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The umpire called the runner out despite loud protests.  尽管抗议声很大，裁判还是判跑垒员出局。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>adversity</b></td><td>/ədˈvɜːrsəti/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>厄运，逆境</td><td>face adversity bravely<br>勇敢面对逆境</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He faced years of adversity without ever losing his sense of humor.  他多年身处逆境，却从未失去幽默感。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ill</td><td>/ˈɪl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>祸害，弊病；生病的</td><td>social ills<br>社会弊病</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The report lists the ills afflicting the health system.  报告列出了困扰医疗系统的种种弊病。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hardship</td><td>/ˈhɑːrdʃɪp/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>艰难，困苦</td><td>endure great hardship<br>忍受巨大艰苦</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The family endured years of hardship after losing their farm.  失去农场后，这家人苦熬了多年。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 misadventure</td><td>/ˌmɪsədˈventʃər/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>不幸，意外的灾祸</td><td>death by misadventure<br>意外致死</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The coroner recorded the death as pure misadventure.  验尸官将该死亡记录为纯属意外。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mischance</td><td>/mɪsˈtʃæns/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>不幸，厄运</td><td>by sheer mischance<br>纯属不幸</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 By sheer mischance the letter arrived a day too late.  纯属不幸，信迟到了一天。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mishap</td><td>/ˈmɪshæp/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>小事故；不幸之事</td><td>a minor mishap<br>小事故</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A minor mishap in the kitchen delayed dinner by nearly an hour.  厨房里的一个小事故让晚饭推迟了将近一小时。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tragedy</td><td>/ˈtrædʒədi/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>惨事，灾难；悲剧</td><td>a terrible tragedy<br>一场可怕的惨剧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The mine collapse was a tragedy that the town never forgot.  矿井塌方是这座小镇永难忘却的惨剧。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 fortune</td><td>/ˈfɔːrtʃən/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>运气；财富</td><td>a stroke of good fortune<br>一次好运</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A stroke of good fortune saved the failing bakery.  一次好运救了这家濒临倒闭的面包店。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 fluke</td><td>/ˈfluːk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>侥幸，意外的好运</td><td>a lucky fluke<br>侥幸的好运</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Winning that first match was a complete fluke.  赢下第一场比赛完全是侥幸。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 luck</td><td>/lʌk/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>运气，幸运</td><td>a run of bad luck<br>一连串坏运气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Pure luck brought them to the only open shelter.  纯凭运气他们找到了唯一开放的避难所。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 serendipity</td><td>/ˌserənˈdɪpəti/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>意外发现珍奇（或称心）事物的本领</td><td>a moment of serendipity<br>意外收获的时刻</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Pure serendipity led the researchers to a cure they were not seeking.  纯粹的意外机缘让研究者找到了他们并未寻求的疗法。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 2

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>affectation</b></td><td>/ˌæfekˈteɪʃn/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>虚伪，做作</td><td>an obvious affectation<br>明显的矫饰做作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His British accent struck everyone as a rather transparent affectation.  在众人看来，他的英式口音是相当明显的做作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 façade</td><td>/fəˈsɑd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>假象，表面；建筑正面</td><td>a façade of calm<br>平静的假象</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Behind a façade of calm, the company was nearly bankrupt.  在平静的表象背后，这家公司已近破产。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 guise</td><td>/ˈɡaɪz/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>外观，伪装的样子</td><td>under the guise of<br>以…为幌子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He entered the palace under the guise of a merchant.  他以商人为幌子进入了宫殿。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mannerism</td><td>/ˈmænərɪzəm/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>习惯性动作，怪癖</td><td>an odd verbal mannerism<br>奇怪的口头习惯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His nervous mannerisms distracted the whole audience.  他紧张的小动作让全场观众分神。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pose</td><td>/ˈpoʊz/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>假装，冒充；姿态</td><td>pose as an expert<br>冒充专家</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He posed as a doctor in order to get into the ward.  他冒充医生以便进入病房。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pretense</td><td>/ˈpriːtens/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>假装，伪装；借口</td><td>under false pretenses<br>以虚假的名义</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He obtained the money under false pretenses and was arrested months later.  他以虚假的名义骗取钱财，几个月后被逮捕。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 show</td><td>/ˈʃoʊ/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>显示，展现；展览</td><td>show your feelings<br>表露感情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He rarely shows his feelings, even to close friends.  他很少表露感情，连对好友也如此。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 artlessness</del></td><td>/ˈɑrtləsnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>率真，毫无心机</td><td>childlike artlessness<br>孩童般的率真</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her artlessness in conversation made strangers trust her quickly.  她谈话中的率真让陌生人很快信任她。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 genuineness</td><td>/ˈdʒenjuɪnnəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>真诚，真实</td><td>the genuineness of her concern<br>她关心的真诚</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 No one in the room doubted the genuineness of her concern.  屋里没人怀疑她关心的真诚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 innocence</td><td>/ˈɪnəsəns/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>天真，纯真；无罪</td><td>childlike innocence<br>孩童般的天真</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She spoke with a childlike innocence that disarmed everyone.  她说话时的孩童般天真让所有人放下戒心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 naivety</td><td>/naɪˈiːvəti/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>天真，幼稚</td><td>political naivety<br>政治上的幼稚</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His political naivety eventually cost him the election.  他政治上的幼稚最终使他输掉了选举。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>affirm</b></td><td>/əˈfɜːrm/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>肯定，断言</td><td>affirm one's belief<br>坚定表明信念</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The witness affirmed that she had seen the blue car.  证人确认她看见了那辆蓝色汽车。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 allege</td><td>/əˈledʒ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>声称，指称（未经证实）</td><td>allege serious misconduct<br>指称严重不当行为</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Prosecutors allege that the manager hid millions from investors.  检方指称该经理向投资者隐匿了数百万资金。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 assert</td><td>/əˈsɜːrt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>断言，声称；维护</td><td>assert one's innocence<br>声称自己无罪</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The defendant continued to assert his innocence throughout the trial.  被告在整个审判过程中不断声称自己无罪。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 aver</td><td>/əˈvɜːr/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>声称为真</td><td>aver that it is true<br>断言此事为真</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The witness averred that he had never met the defendant before.  证人声明他此前从未见过被告。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 avouch</del></td><td>/əˈvaʊtʃ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>断言，保证，证实</td><td>avouch the truth of it<br>保证其真实性</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two witnesses avouched the accuracy of the merchant's account.  两名证人证实了这位商人陈述的准确性。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 avow</td><td>/əˈvaʊ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>公开承认，坦率声明</td><td>avow one's beliefs openly<br>公开表明信念</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He avowed his support for the strike in front of reporters.  他在记者面前公开表明支持这次罢工。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 contend</td><td>/kənˈtend/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词、及物动词</td><td>声称，主张；争夺</td><td>contend that the claim is false<br>声称该说法不实</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lawyer contended that the evidence had been obtained illegally.  律师声称这些证据是非法取得的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 declare</td><td>/dɪˈkler/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>宣布，声明，断言</td><td>declare one's intentions<br>表明自己的意图</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge declared the earlier verdict invalid and ordered a retrial.  法官宣布先前判决无效并下令重审。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 insist</td><td>/ɪnˈsɪst/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>坚持，坚决主张</td><td>insist on an apology<br>坚持要求道歉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She insisted that the money had been returned last week.  她坚称那笔钱上周已经归还。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 maintain</td><td>/meɪnˈteɪn/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>坚持主张；维持，保养</td><td>maintain his innocence<br>坚称自己无罪</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He continued to maintain that he had never seen the document.  他一直坚称自己从未见过那份文件。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 profess</td><td>/prəˈfes/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>声称，假称</td><td>profess ignorance<br>声称不知情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He professed great sympathy but offered no actual help.  他声称非常同情，却没提供实际帮助。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 protest</td><td>/ˈproʊˌtest/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>抗议，反对</td><td>register a formal protest<br>提出正式抗议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The ambassador registered a formal protest with the foreign ministry.  大使向外交部提出正式抗议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 purport</td><td>/pərˈpɔːrt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>声称，打算</td><td>purport to be an expert<br>自称是专家</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Several websites purport to offer free medical advice from real doctors.  有几个网站声称提供真医生的免费医疗建议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 warrant</td><td>/ˈwɔːrənt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>保证，担保；使有正当理由</td><td>warrant the quality<br>保证质量</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The factory warrants the quality of every tool it sells abroad.  这家工厂为其出口的每件工具的质量提供保证。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 deny</td><td>/dɪˈnaɪ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>否认；拒绝给予</td><td>flatly deny the charge<br>断然否认指控</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 I do not deny that the plan carries some very real risks.  我不否认这个计划确实带有一些实实在在的风险。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 gainsay</td><td>/ˌɡeɪnˈseɪ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>否认</td><td>gainsay the evidence<br>否认证据</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 No one could gainsay the evidence the inspector had collected.  没有人能否认检查员收集到的证据。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 disclaim</td><td>/dɪsˈkleɪm/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>否认，拒绝承认</td><td>disclaim responsibility<br>否认责任</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company quickly disclaimed any link to the failed venture.  公司迅速否认与那个失败项目有任何关联。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 recant</td><td>/rɪˈkænt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>撤回，公开放弃（说法）</td><td>recant a confession<br>撤回供词</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Under pressure he agreed to recant his earlier statement.  在压力下他同意撤回先前的声明。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>affix</b></td><td>/əˈfɪks/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>贴上，附加</td><td>affix a stamp<br>贴上邮票</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Please affix a stamp to the corner of the envelope before mailing it.  寄出之前请在信封角上贴一张邮票。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 attach</td><td>/əˈtætʃ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>附上，粘上；使依附</td><td>attach a label<br>贴上标签</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Please attach a label to each box before shipping it.  发货前请在每个箱子上贴标签。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bend</td><td>/ˈbend/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>使弯曲；弯道</td><td>bend the metal rod<br>弯折金属棒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He tried to bend the metal rod to fit the narrow frame.  他试着把金属棒弯折，以便装进窄框。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fix</td><td>/ˈfɪks/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>修理，解决；固定</td><td>fix the leaking pipe<br>修好漏水的管子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A neighbor fixed the leaking pipe before the floor was ruined.  邻居在地板毁坏前修好了漏水的管子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 detach</td><td>/dɪˈtætʃ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使分离</td><td>detach the coupon carefully<br>小心撕下优惠券</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Carefully detach the label before you put the jar in water.  把罐子放进水里之前先小心地把标签撕下来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 undo</td><td>/ʌnˈduː/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>解开，松开；取消</td><td>undo the buttons<br>解开纽扣</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He undid the buttons of his heavy coat and sat down.  他解开厚外套的纽扣坐了下来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unfasten</td><td>/ˌʌnˈfæsən/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>解开，松开</td><td>unfasten a seat belt<br>解开安全带</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Passengers may unfasten their seat belts once the plane lands.  飞机着陆后乘客可以解开安全带。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unhook</td><td>/ʌnˈhʊk/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>解开挂钩，摘下</td><td>unhook the trailer<br>解开拖车挂钩</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He unhooked the trailer and drove away without a word.  他解开拖车挂钩，一句话没说就开走了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>affliction</b></td><td>/əˈflɪkʃn/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>痛苦，悲伤，折磨</td><td>suffer a lifelong affliction<br>承受终生的痛苦</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Chronic back pain was an affliction she learned to live with quietly.  慢性背痛是她学会默默承受的一种折磨。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 agony</td><td>/ˈæɡəni/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>极度痛苦</td><td>in agony<br>处于极度痛苦中</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She lay in agony on the floor until the ambulance arrived.  她在地上极度痛苦地躺着直到救护车到来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 anguish</td><td>/ˈæŋɡwɪʃ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>极度痛苦，苦恼</td><td>in great anguish<br>极度痛苦之中</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His face showed the anguish of a sleepless night.  他的脸上写着一夜无眠的痛苦。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dolor</td><td>/ˈdoʊlər/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>悲痛，哀伤</td><td>deep dolor<br>深切的悲痛</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His later poems are filled with the dolor of exile.  他后期的诗充满流亡的悲痛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 excruciation</del></td><td>/ɪkˌskruːʃiˈeɪʃən/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>极度痛苦，折磨</td><td>sheer excruciation<br>十足的折磨</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The last mile of the race was sheer excruciation for her.  比赛最后一英里对她纯粹是折磨。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 grief</td><td>/ˈɡriːf/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>悲痛，哀伤</td><td>overcome with grief<br>悲痛不已</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She was silent with grief for weeks after her father died.  父亲去世后，她数周沉默悲痛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hurt</td><td>/ˈhɜːrt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>伤害，使疼痛</td><td>hurt his feelings<br>伤害他的感情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Your careless comment clearly hurt her feelings at the dinner.  你随口的评论显然在晚餐上伤了她的感情。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 misery</td><td>/ˈmɪzəri/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>痛苦，苦难</td><td>live in misery<br>生活在痛苦中</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The war brought years of misery to ordinary families.  战争给普通家庭带来了多年的苦难。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pain</td><td>/ˈpeɪn/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>麻烦事；疼痛</td><td>a pain in the neck<br>讨厌的麻烦</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Filling out these forms is a pain, but skipping them costs more.  填这些表格很烦人，但不填代价更大。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rack</td><td>/ˈræk/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>折磨，使痛苦；架子</td><td>racked with pain<br>受痛苦折磨</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was racked by guilt long after the incident was forgotten.  事件被淡忘后他仍长久受负罪感折磨。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 torment</td><td>/tɔːrˈment/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>折磨，使痛苦；痛苦</td><td>torment the prisoners<br>折磨囚犯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Guilt tormented him for years after the accident.  事故之后，负罪感折磨了他多年。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 torture</td><td>/ˈtɔːrtʃər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>折磨，拷打；酷刑</td><td>under torture<br>在酷刑之下</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The long wait for results was pure torture for her.  漫长等待结果对她来说简直是折磨。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 travail</td><td>/ˈtræveɪl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>辛苦劳动</td><td>years of travail<br>多年辛劳</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After months of travail, the small team finally finished the bridge.  经过数月的辛苦劳作，这个小团队终于建成了这座桥。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tribulation</td><td>/ˌtrɪbjəˈleɪʃən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>苦难，忧患</td><td>trials and tribulations<br>种种艰难困苦</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They survived years of tribulation in the refugee camp.  他们在难民营中度过了多年苦难。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 woe</td><td>/ˈwoʊ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>悲哀，苦难</td><td>a tale of woe<br>一段悲惨遭遇</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He told us a long tale of woe about his debts.  他向我们讲了一段关于债务的漫长悲惨遭遇。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 bliss</td><td>/blɪs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>极度快乐</td><td>domestic bliss<br>家庭幸福</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They spent a year of quiet domestic bliss in the countryside.  他们在乡下度过了一年宁静而极度幸福的家庭生活。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 cheer</td><td>/ˈtʃɪr/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>欢呼，喝彩；使高兴</td><td>a loud cheer<br>一阵响亮的欢呼</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A loud cheer rose from the crowd when the goal was scored.  进球时人群中响起一阵响亮的欢呼。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 delight</td><td>/dɪˈlaɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>使高兴，使愉快；愉悦</td><td>delight the audience<br>使观众高兴</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The young pianist delighted the audience with a lively encore.  这位年轻钢琴家用一首活泼的加演曲取悦了观众。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ecstasy</td><td>/ˈekstəsi/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>狂喜</td><td>in a state of ecstasy<br>处于狂喜之中</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The crowd rose in a state of ecstasy when the winning goal went in.  制胜球进网时，全场观众狂喜地站了起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 elation</td><td>/ɪˈleɪʃən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>得意，兴高采烈</td><td>a feeling of elation<br>兴高采烈之感</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her elation faded once she read the fine print carefully.  她仔细读了小字条款后，兴奋就消退了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 euphoria</td><td>/juːˈfɔːriə/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>极度愉快，狂喜</td><td>a mood of national euphoria<br>全国上下的狂喜情绪</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The euphoria after the victory faded within a couple of days.  胜利后的极度愉快在几天之内就消退了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 exhilaration</td><td>/ɪɡˌzɪləˈreɪʃən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>兴奋，极度愉快</td><td>a sense of exhilaration<br>一种兴奋感</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She felt pure exhilaration as the glider rose above the valley.  滑翔机升到山谷上方时，她感到无比兴奋。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 exuberance</td><td>/ɪɡˈzuːbərəns/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>热情洋溢，兴高采烈</td><td>youthful exuberance<br>年轻人的热情洋溢</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The band played with an exuberance that lifted the whole room.  乐队演奏得热情洋溢，带动了满屋气氛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 exultation</td><td>/ˌeɡzʌlˈteɪʃən/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>狂喜，欢欣</td><td>a cry of exultation<br>一声欢呼</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A cry of exultation went up when the winning goal was scored.  制胜球进网时爆发出一声欢呼。</td></tr>

<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 felicity</td><td>/fɪˈlɪsəti/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>得体，恰当；幸福</td><td>felicity of expression<br>措辞得体</td></tr>

<tr><td>↳反 joy</td><td>/ˈdʒɔɪ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>欢乐，喜悦</td><td>jump for joy<br>高兴得跳起来</td></tr>

<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 jubilation</td><td>/ˌdʒuːbəˈleɪʃən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>欢腾，喜悦</td><td>scenes of jubilation<br>欢腾的场面</td></tr>

<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 pleasure</td><td>/ˈpleʒər/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>愉快，快乐</td><td>take pleasure in reading<br>以阅读为乐</td></tr>

<tr><td>↳反 rapture</td><td>/ˈræptʃər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>极度欢喜，狂喜</td><td>listen in rapture<br>如痴如醉地聆听</td></tr>

<tbody>
<tr><td><b>agape</b></td><td>/əˈɡeɪp/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>目瞪口呆的；张大嘴的</td><td>stand there agape<br>张口结舌地站着</td></tr>

<tr><td>↳近 agog</td><td>/əˈɡɑːɡ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>极度感兴趣的，兴奋期待的</td><td>all agog with excitement<br>兴奋不已，满心期待</td></tr>

<tr><td colspan="7">例 The interrogators excruciated the prisoners for information they never had.  审讯者折磨囚犯，逼问他们从未掌握的情报。</td></tr>

<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 harrow</td><td>/ˈhæroʊ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>折磨，使苦恼</td><td>harrow his conscience<br>折磨他的良心</td></tr>

<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 torture</td><td>/ˈtɔːrtʃər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>折磨，拷打；酷刑</td><td>under torture<br>在酷刑之下</td></tr>

</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 comfort</td><td>/ˈkʌmfərt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>舒适；安慰</td><td>live in comfort<br>过舒适的生活</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old chair gave him a great deal of comfort.  那把旧椅子给他带来极大的舒适。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 console</td><td>/kənˈsoʊl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>安慰，慰藉</td><td>console a grieving friend<br>安慰悲伤的朋友</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Nothing anyone said could console her that evening.  那天晚上无论谁说什么都无法安慰她。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 solace</td><td>/ˈsɑːləs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>安慰</td><td>find solace in music<br>在音乐中找到安慰</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She found solace in music during the long months of her illness.  在漫长的病中，她在音乐里找到安慰。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 soothe</td><td>/suːð/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>安慰，抚慰；减轻</td><td>soothe a crying baby<br>安抚哭闹的婴儿</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Soft music helped soothe the restless patient through the long night.  轻柔的音乐帮助安抚了焦躁的病人度过漫长的夜晚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>agreeable</b></td><td>/əˈɡriːəbl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>令人愉悦的；相一致的</td><td>an agreeable companion<br>一个讨人喜欢的同伴</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She found him an agreeable companion on the long train journey.  她觉得他是长途火车旅程中一位讨人喜欢的同伴。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 congenial</td><td>/kənˈdʒiːniəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>和善的，友好的</td><td>a congenial host<br>一位和善的主人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Our congenial host made every guest feel completely at home.  我们和善的主人让每位客人都感到自在。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 delectable</td><td>/dɪˈlektəbəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>美味的；令人愉悦的</td><td>a delectable dessert<br>美味的甜点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She served a delectable dessert made from summer berries.  她端上用夏日莓果做的美味甜点。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 delicious</td><td>/dɪˈlɪʃəs/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>美味的，可口的</td><td>a delicious meal<br>一顿美味的饭</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The soup was so delicious that nobody left a drop.  这汤太可口，没人剩下一滴。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 delightful</td><td>/dɪˈlaɪtfəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>令人愉快的，宜人的</td><td>a delightful surprise<br>令人愉快的惊喜</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We spent a delightful evening by the lake.  我们在湖边度过了一个愉快的夜晚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dulcet</td><td>/ˈdʌlsɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>悦耳的，令人愉悦的</td><td>dulcet tones<br>悦耳的音调</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her dulcet tones calmed the restless children within a couple of minutes.  她悦耳的音调几分钟内就安抚了不安的孩子们。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 enjoyable</td><td>/ɪnˈdʒɔɪəbəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>令人愉快的，有趣的</td><td>an enjoyable evening<br>愉快的一晚</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We had a thoroughly enjoyable evening at the concert.  我们在音乐会上度过了非常愉快的一晚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 felicitous</td><td>/fɪˈlɪsətəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>恰当的，贴切的</td><td>a felicitous phrase<br>贴切的措辞</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The title is a felicitous choice for such a book.  这个书名对这样一本书来说是贴切的选择。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 gratifying</td><td>/ˈɡrætəˌfaɪɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>令人满意的，令人欣慰的</td><td>a gratifying result<br>令人满意的结果</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It was gratifying to see her hard work finally rewarded.  看到她的努力终获回报令人欣慰。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 heavenly</td><td>/ˈhevənli/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>天上的；美妙无比的</td><td>heavenly bodies<br>天体</td></tr>

<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 palatable</td><td>/ˈpælətəbl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>美味的；可接受的</td><td>a palatable compromise<br>令人满意的妥协</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The chef made the healthy vegetables surprisingly palatable to the children.  厨师把健康的蔬菜做得让孩子们意外地喜欢。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pleasing</td><td>/ˈpliːzɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>悦耳的，令人愉快的</td><td>a pleasing melody<br>悦耳的旋律</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her voice carried a pleasing warmth that calmed the children.  她的声音带着悦耳的温暖，让孩子们安静下来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 satisfying</td><td>/ˈsætɪsˌfaɪɪŋ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>令人满意的；有说服力的</td><td>a satisfying explanation<br>令人满意的解释</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 No one offered a satisfying answer to the reporter's question.  没人对记者的问题给出令人满意的答复。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 savory</td><td>/ˈseɪvəri/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>美味的，可口的</td><td>a savory dish<br>一道可口的菜</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She served a savory pie made with mushrooms and herbs.  她端上一道用蘑菇和香草做的美味派。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 amicable</td><td>/ˈæmɪkəbl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>友善的，和睦的</td><td>an amicable settlement<br>友好的和解</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The dispute ended in an amicable agreement between neighbors.  这场纠纷以邻里间的友好协议告终。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 compatible</td><td>/kəmˈpætəbl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>一致的，能共存的</td><td>compatible with the system<br>与该系统兼容</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new driver is compatible with both older printers.  新驱动与两款旧打印机都兼容。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 consistent</td><td>/kənˈsɪstənt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>一致的；始终如一的</td><td>consistent with the evidence<br>与证据相符</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His account is consistent with what the neighbors reported.  他的说法与邻居们所述一致。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 consonant</td><td>/ˈkɑːnsənənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>和谐一致的</td><td>consonant with tradition<br>与传统相符</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His modest lifestyle is consonant with the values he preaches.  他朴素的生活方式与他宣扬的价值观相符。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 frictionless</td><td>/ˈfrɪkʃənləs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无摩擦的，顺畅融洽的</td><td>a frictionless partnership<br>融洽顺畅的合作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two departments enjoy a frictionless working relationship.  两个部门保持着顺畅融洽的工作关系。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 kindred</td><td>/ˈkɪndrəd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>同类的，相似的</td><td>a kindred spirit<br>志趣相投的人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We recognized each other as kindred spirits within the first five minutes.  头五分钟我们就认出彼此是志趣相投的人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unanimous</td><td>/juˈnænɪməs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>一致同意的</td><td>a unanimous decision<br>一致通过的决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The jury reached a unanimous verdict after only two hours of discussion.  陪审团仅讨论两小时便达成了一致的裁决。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 united</td><td>/juˈnaɪtɪd/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>联合的，团结一致的</td><td>a united front<br>统一战线</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The villages stayed united against the plan to divert the river.  各村庄团结一致反对改道河流的计划。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 abhorrent</td><td>/æbˈhɔːrənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>令人厌恶的</td><td>abhorrent to most people<br>令多数人厌恶</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The practice is abhorrent to anyone with basic decency.  这种做法令任何有基本良知的人厌恶。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 abominable</td><td>/əˈbɑːmənəbəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>可憎的，极糟的</td><td>abominable behavior<br>可憎的行为</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His abominable behavior at dinner shocked the guests.  他晚餐时可憎的举止让客人震惊。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 disagreeable</td><td>/ˌdɪsəˈɡriːəbəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>令人不快的；脾气坏的</td><td>a disagreeable task<br>令人不快的任务</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Cleaning the drains was a thoroughly disagreeable job.  清理下水道是件极其讨人厌的活儿。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 disgusting</td><td>/dɪsˈɡʌstɪŋ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>令人厌恶的，恶心的</td><td>a disgusting smell<br>令人恶心的气味</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The kitchen was left in a disgusting state after the party.  聚会后厨房脏得让人恶心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 loathsome</td><td>/ˈloʊðsəm/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>令人厌恶的</td><td>a loathsome smell<br>令人厌恶的气味</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A loathsome smell rose from the neglected drain.  无人清理的下水道升起令人厌恶的气味。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 nauseating</td><td>/ˈnɔːziˌeɪtɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>令人恶心的</td><td>a nauseating stench<br>令人恶心的臭味</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The nauseating stench drove everyone out of the kitchen.  令人恶心的臭味把所有人赶出厨房。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 repellent</td><td>/rɪˈpelənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>令人厌恶的；驱虫剂</td><td>a repellent odor<br>令人厌恶的气味</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 I found his arrogant remarks utterly repellent and left early.  我觉得他傲慢的言论令人极其厌恶，于是提前离开。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 repugnant</td><td>/rɪˈpʌɡnənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>令人厌恶的</td><td>morally repugnant behavior<br>道德上可憎的行为</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Most readers found the cruelty of that character morally repugnant and unforgivable.  多数读者觉得那个角色的残忍在道德上令人厌恶，不可宽恕。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 repulsive</td><td>/rɪˈpʌlsɪv/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>令人厌恶的，恶心的</td><td>a repulsive sight<br>令人恶心的景象</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The rotting fish on the beach was absolutely repulsive.  沙滩上腐烂的鱼令人极其恶心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 revolting</td><td>/rɪˈvoʊltɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>令人厌恶的，恶心的</td><td>a revolting smell<br>令人恶心的气味</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A revolting smell came from the abandoned refrigerator in the yard.  院里那台废弃冰箱散出一股恶心的气味。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 discordant</td><td>/dɪˈskɔːrdənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不一致的，不和谐的</td><td>discordant opinions<br>不一致的意见</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Discordant voices from the back ruined the quiet ceremony.  后面不和谐的声音破坏了安静的仪式。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 disunited</td><td>/ˌdɪsjuˈnaɪtɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>分裂的，不团结的</td><td>a disunited party<br>分裂的政党</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A disunited opposition could not challenge the ruling coalition.  分裂的反对派无法挑战执政联盟。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 incompatible</td><td>/ˌɪnkəmˈpætəbəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不相容的，矛盾的</td><td>incompatible goals<br>互不相容的目标</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Rapid growth and strict conservation seemed incompatible goals here.  在这里快速发展与严格保护似乎是矛盾的目标。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 inharmonious</del></td><td>/ˌɪnhɑːrˈmoʊniəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不和谐的，不协调的</td><td>inharmonious colors<br>不协调的颜色</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new wing looked inharmonious beside the old stone chapel.  新翼楼在古老石砌小教堂旁显得不协调。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>alienate</b></td><td>/ˈeɪliəneɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>疏远，离间；使疏离</td><td>alienate one's supporters<br>疏远自己的支持者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His harsh remarks alienated many longtime friends within a single evening.  他刻薄的言辞在一个晚上就疏远了许多老朋友。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 alien</td><td>/ˈeɪliən/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>外来的，陌生的</td><td>alien to our culture<br>与我们的文化格格不入</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Such customs seemed completely alien to the newcomers.  这些习俗对新来者来说完全陌生。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 disaffect</del></td><td>/ˌdɪsəˈfekt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使不满，使离心</td><td>disaffected voters<br>心生不满的选民</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The broken promises disaffected much of the party base.  这些空头承诺使党的许多基础支持者离心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 disgruntle</del></td><td>/dɪsˈɡrʌntl/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使不满，使发怒</td><td>disgruntle the staff<br>使员工不满</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The sudden pay freeze disgruntled the entire department.  突然的薪资冻结让整个部门都不满。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sour</td><td>/ˈsaʊər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、形容词</td><td>使恶化，使变坏；酸的</td><td>sour the relationship<br>使关系恶化</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The argument soured their friendship for years to come.  那次争吵使他们的友谊多年蒙上阴影。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 reconcile</td><td>/ˈrekənsaɪl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使和解，协调</td><td>reconcile conflicting views<br>调和相互冲突的观点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It is hard to reconcile these two versions of the story.  很难把这个故事的两种说法协调一致。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>alloy</b></td><td>/ˈæˌlɔɪ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>合金；使（品质）降低，掺杂</td><td>an alloy of copper and tin<br>铜锡合金</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Bronze is an alloy of copper and tin used since ancient times.  青铜是自古就使用的铜锡合金。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 admixture</td><td>/ædˈmɪkstʃər/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>混合物，掺和料</td><td>an admixture of clay<br>掺入的黏土</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The concrete contains an admixture that slows the drying process.  这种混凝土含有一种延缓干燥的掺和料。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 amalgam</td><td>/əˈmælɡəm/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>混合物；汞合金</td><td>an amalgam of styles<br>多种风格的混合</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her music is an amalgam of jazz and traditional folk song.  她的音乐是爵士与传统民歌的混合。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cocktail</td><td>/ˈkɑːkˌteɪl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>混合物；鸡尾酒</td><td>a cocktail of drugs<br>多种药物的混合</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Patients receive a cocktail of three drugs each morning.  病人每天早上服用三种药物的混合剂。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 composite</td><td>/kəmˈpɑːzət/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>合成的；合成物</td><td>a composite picture<br>合成的图像</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The artist built a composite portrait from four separate photographs.  艺术家用四张照片合成了一幅肖像。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 compound</td><td>/kəmˈpaʊnd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>混合，合成；使恶化</td><td>compound a medicine<br>配制药剂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The pharmacist compounded an ointment from three simple ingredients.  药剂师用三种简单原料配制出一种药膏。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fusion</td><td>/ˈfjuːʒən/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>融合，结合；熔合</td><td>a fusion of cultures<br>文化的融合</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their cooking is a fusion of Korean and Peruvian traditions.  他们的烹饪是韩国与秘鲁传统的融合。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 intermixture</del></td><td>/ˌɪntərˈmɪkstʃər/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>混合，混合物</td><td>an intermixture of peoples<br>各族群的混居</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The port city grew from an intermixture of many trading peoples.  这座港城由许多贸易族群混居发展而来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mix</td><td>/ˈmɪks/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>混合，掺和</td><td>mix the ingredients<br>把配料混合</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Mix the flour and water until the dough becomes smooth.  把面粉和水混合，直到面团变得光滑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mixture</td><td>/ˈmɪkstʃər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>混合物，混合体</td><td>a mixture of styles<br>风格的混合</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The soup is a mixture of beans, corn and smoked meat.  这汤是豆子、玉米和熏肉的混合。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 synthesis</td><td>/ˈsɪnθəsɪs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>合成，综合</td><td>a synthesis of ideas<br>各种思想的综合</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His theory is a synthesis of ideas drawn from several disciplines.  他的理论是多个学科思想的综合。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 adulterate</td><td>/əˈdʌltəreɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>掺杂，加入低等成分</td><td>adulterate milk with water<br>在牛奶里掺水</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The dealer was fined for adulterating olive oil with cheaper fat.  这个商人因在橄榄油中掺入廉价油脂而被罚款。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 contaminate</td><td>/kənˈtæmɪneɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>污染，感染</td><td>contaminate the water supply<br>污染供水</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Leaking chemicals contaminated the soil around the factory.  泄漏的化学品污染了工厂周围的土壤。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dilute</td><td>/daɪˈluːt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词、形容词</td><td>稀释，冲淡；经稀释的</td><td>dilute the acid<br>稀释这种酸</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Please dilute the concentrated juice with three parts water before serving it.  上桌前请用三倍的水稀释这种浓缩果汁。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 extend</td><td>/ɪkˈstend/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>延长，扩展</td><td>extend a deadline<br>延长截止期限</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They agreed to extend the deadline by two weeks.  他们同意把截止日期延长两周。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lace</td><td>/ˈleɪs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>猛击；系带</td><td>lace into an opponent<br>猛烈攻击对手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The boxer laced into his rival in the final round.  拳手在最后一轮猛击对手。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pollute</td><td>/pəˈluːt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>污染，弄脏</td><td>pollute the environment<br>污染环境</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Factories along the river have polluted the water for decades.  沿河的工厂几十年来一直在污染水体。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sophisticate</td><td>/səˈfɪstəˌkeɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>使复杂化；老于世故者</td><td>sophisticate the design further<br>把设计进一步复杂化</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Engineers sophisticated the model until nobody could explain it.  工程师把模型改得越来越复杂，最后没人能解释清楚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 thin</td><td>/ˈθɪn/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词、形容词</td><td>使变稀；薄的，瘦的</td><td>thin the paint slightly<br>把颜料稍微稀释</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Thin the sauce with a little warm water before serving.  上菜前用少量温水把酱汁调稀。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 element</td><td>/ˈeləmənt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>要素，成分；元素</td><td>a key element<br>关键要素</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Trust is a key element in any lasting partnership.  信任是任何持久合作关系的关键要素。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 purify</td><td>/ˈpjʊrəˌfaɪ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>净化，使纯净</td><td>purify the water<br>净化水质</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 These tablets purify water so it becomes safe to drink.  这些药片能净化水，使其可安全饮用。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 3

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>ambivalent</b></td><td>/æmˈbɪvələnt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>（态度、感情）矛盾的，摇摆不定的</td><td>ambivalent about the offer<br>对该提议态度矛盾</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Many voters remain ambivalent about the new tax proposal.  许多选民对新的税收提案态度依然矛盾。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 conflicting</td><td>/kənˈflɪktɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>冲突的，矛盾的</td><td>conflicting accounts<br>相互矛盾的说法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Witnesses gave conflicting accounts of the driver's speed.  证人对司机车速的说法相互矛盾。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 contradictory</td><td>/ˌkɑːntrəˈdɪktəri/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>相互矛盾的</td><td>contradictory evidence<br>相互矛盾的证据</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The witnesses gave contradictory accounts of the accident.  证人们对事故给出了相互矛盾的说法。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mixed</td><td>/ˈmɪkst/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>混合的，掺杂的；好坏交织的</td><td>mixed feelings<br>复杂的心情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 I have mixed feelings about moving abroad.  对于出国我心情复杂。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 certain</td><td>/ˈsɜːrtən/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>确定的，无疑的</td><td>certain of the outcome<br>对结果有把握</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We are certain that the letter was never sent.  我们确信那封信从未寄出。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 decided</td><td>/dɪˈsaɪdɪd/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>坚决的，明确的</td><td>a decided advantage<br>明显的优势</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She spoke in a decided tone that ended all discussion.  她用坚决的语气说话，结束了所有讨论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 definite</td><td>/ˈdefənət/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>明确的，确定的</td><td>a definite answer<br>明确的答复</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We need a definite answer before we can book the venue.  我们需要明确答复才能订场地。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 positive</td><td>/ˈpɑːzətɪv/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>确定的，明确的；积极的</td><td>positive proof<br>确凿的证据</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 I am positive that I locked the back door.  我确定我锁了后门。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 resolute</td><td>/ˈrezəluːt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>坚定的</td><td>remain resolute<br>保持坚定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Despite heavy criticism, the mayor remained resolute about closing the old factory.  尽管备受批评，市长在关闭旧工厂一事上依然坚定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
