<tr><td>↳反 disliked</td><td>/dɪsˈlaɪkt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不受喜欢的，遭反感的</td><td>a widely disliked policy<br>普遍不受欢迎的政策</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new manager was disliked by almost all of his staff.  新经理几乎不受任何下属喜欢。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 execrated</del></td><td>/ˈɛksəkreɪtɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词、动词</td><td>被咒骂的，遭痛斥的</td><td>an execrated tyrant<br>遭人诅咒的暴君</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His name was execrated in every newspaper the following morning.  第二天早上每份报纸都在痛骂他的名字。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 hated</td><td>/ˈheɪtəd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>被憎恨的</td><td>the most hated man<br>最遭人恨的人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He became the most hated figure in the entire industry.  他成了整个行业里最遭人憎恨的人物。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 loathed</td><td>/ˈloʊðd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>被厌恶的，遭嫌恶的</td><td>a loathed enemy<br>憎恶的敌人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The loathed old rule was finally scrapped last year.  那条令人厌恶的旧规定去年终于被废除。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>chivalrous</b></td><td>/ˈʃɪvəlrəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有骑士风度的，（尤指对女性）彬彬有礼的</td><td>a chivalrous gesture<br>有骑士风度的举动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He made a chivalrous offer to carry her heavy suitcase.  他很有骑士风度地提出替她拿沉重的行李箱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 big</td><td>/ˈbɪɡ/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>大量的，大幅的；重要的</td><td>a big salary increase<br>大幅加薪</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She was offered a big raise and took the job at once.  她获得大幅加薪，立刻接受了这份工作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 elevated</td><td>/ˈeləˌveɪtɪd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>高尚的，崇高的；升高的</td><td>elevated language<br>高雅的语言</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The speech used elevated language that moved the crowd.  这篇演讲用高雅的语言感动了人群。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 gallant</td><td>/ˈɡælənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>英勇的</td><td>a gallant defense<br>英勇的防守</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The gallant paratroopers jumped from the plane without a moment's hesitation.  英勇的伞兵们毫不犹豫地跳出了飞机。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 great</td><td>/ˈɡreɪt/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>显赫的，地位高的</td><td>the great families<br>显赫的家族</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The great families of the city controlled every guild.  城中的显赫家族控制着每个行会。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 greathearted</del></td><td>/ˌɡreɪtˈhɑːrtɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>宽宏大量的，勇敢的</td><td>a greathearted friend<br>宽宏大量的朋友</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her greathearted forgiveness surprised even her critics.  她宽宏的原谅甚至让批评者惊讶。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 high</td><td>/ˈhaɪ/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>高尚的，崇高的</td><td>high ideals<br>崇高的理想</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He held himself to high moral standards throughout his long career.  他整个漫长的职业生涯都以高尚的道德标准自律。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 high-minded</td><td>/ˌhaɪˈmaɪndɪd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>品格高尚的，有原则的</td><td>high-minded principles<br>高尚的原则</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His high-minded refusal of the bribe cost him the job.  他高尚地拒绝贿赂，因此失去了工作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lofty</td><td>/ˈlɔːfti/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>崇高的；自大的</td><td>a lofty tone<br>自大的语气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She dismissed our idea with a lofty wave of her hand.  她自大地一挥手就否定了我们的想法。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lordly</td><td>/ˈlɔːrdli/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>傲慢的；高贵的</td><td>a lordly manner<br>傲慢的举止</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He waved the waiter away with a lordly gesture.  他以傲慢的手势把侍者挥退。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 magnanimous</td><td>/mæɡˈnænəməs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>宽宏大量的，大度的</td><td>a magnanimous concession<br>大度的让步</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She was magnanimous in victory and praised her rival.  她胜而不骄，还称赞了对手。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 natural</td><td>/ˈnætʃərəl/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>自然的，正常的</td><td>a natural reaction<br>自然的反应</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It is natural to feel nervous before a big exam.  大考前紧张是很自然的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sublime</td><td>/səˈblaɪm/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>崇高的；壮丽的；卓越的</td><td>a sublime landscape<br>崇高壮丽的景色</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 From the summit we watched a sublime landscape unfold below us.  从山顶我们看着一片壮丽的景色在下方展开。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 base</td><td>/ˈbeɪs/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>卑劣的，低下的</td><td>base motives<br>卑劣的动机</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He acted from base motives of greed and envy.  他出于贪婪和嫉妒的卑劣动机行事。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 debased</td><td>/dəˈbeɪst/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>堕落的，贬损的</td><td>debased taste<br>低劣的品味</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He complained about the debased state of public debate.  他抱怨公共辩论的堕落状态。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 degenerate</td><td>/dɪˈdʒenəreɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词、不及物动词</td><td>衰退的，堕落的；恶化</td><td>degenerate into chaos<br>恶化为混乱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The peaceful protest began to degenerate into shouting and shoving.  和平抗议开始恶化为叫骂和推挤。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 degraded</td><td>/dɪˈɡreɪdəd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>堕落的，卑下的；退化的</td><td>a degraded lifestyle<br>堕落的生活方式</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The novel depicts a once proud family in degraded circumstances.  小说描写了一个曾经显赫的家族沦落的境况。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ignoble</td><td>/ɪɡˈnoʊbəl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>卑鄙的，不高尚的</td><td>an ignoble motive<br>卑劣的动机</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His generosity hid an ignoble desire for public praise.  他的慷慨背后藏着对公众赞誉的卑劣渴望。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ignominious</td><td>/ˌɪɡnəˈmɪniəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>可耻的，不光彩的</td><td>an ignominious defeat<br>可耻的失败</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The season ended in ignominious defeat at home.  这个赛季以主场可耻的失利告终。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 low</td><td>/ˈloʊ/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词、副词</td><td>低落的，沮丧的；低的</td><td>feel a bit low<br>感觉有点低落</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She has been feeling low since her closest friend moved away.  自从最好的朋友搬走，她一直情绪低落。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>chorale</b></td><td>/kəˈrɑːl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>合唱班；合唱圣咏</td><td>sing a chorale<br>唱一首圣咏</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The village chorale sang three hymns at the evening service.  村里的合唱团在晚间礼拜上唱了三首圣歌。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 anthem</td><td>/ˈænθəm/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>圣歌，赞美诗；国歌</td><td>the national anthem<br>国歌</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The crowd stood and sang the anthem before the match began.  比赛开始前人们起立唱国歌。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 canticle</td><td>/ˈkæntɪkəl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>圣歌，颂歌</td><td>an evening canticle<br>晚祷颂歌</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The monks chanted a canticle at the close of the service.  修士们在礼拜结束时唱了一首颂歌。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 carol</td><td>/ˈkærəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>欢快颂唱，歌颂；颂歌</td><td>carol his praises<br>歌颂他的功绩</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The children caroled cheerfully outside every door on the street.  孩子们在街上每户门前欢快地颂唱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hymn</td><td>/hɪm/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>赞歌，赞美诗</td><td>sing a hymn<br>唱赞美诗</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The whole congregation stood up to sing a hymn of thanksgiving.  全体教众站起身来唱一首感恩的赞美诗。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 paean</td><td>/ˈpiːən/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>欢乐颂，赞扬</td><td>a paean to freedom<br>对自由的赞歌</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The review read like a paean to the young violinist and her talent.  那篇评论读来像是对这位年轻小提琴手及其才华的赞歌。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 psalm</td><td>/ˈsɑːm/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>圣歌，赞美诗</td><td>read a psalm aloud<br>朗读一首圣歌</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old man read a psalm before every evening meal.  老人每顿晚餐前都读一首圣歌。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 spiritual</td><td>/ˈspɪrɪtʃuəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>精神的；灵魂的，宗教的</td><td>spiritual growth<br>精神成长</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She turned to meditation in search of spiritual peace.  她转向冥想，以寻求精神上的平静。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 choir</td><td>/ˈkwaɪər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>唱诗班，合唱团</td><td>join the church choir<br>加入教堂唱诗班</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She has sung in the church choir since she was eight.  她从八岁起就在教堂唱诗班里唱歌。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 chorus</td><td>/ˈkɔːrəs/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、不及物动词</td><td>合唱，齐声；合唱队</td><td>sing in chorus<br>齐声合唱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The villagers answered the question in chorus.  村民们齐声回答了这个问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 consort</td><td>/kənˈsɔːrt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>结交</td><td>consort with criminals<br>与罪犯结交</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 No respectable official would consort with smugglers so openly in public.  没有正派的官员会如此公开地与走私者结交。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ensemble</td><td>/ɑːnˈsɑːmbəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>合奏团，全体；整体</td><td>a small string ensemble<br>小型弦乐合奏团</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A small ensemble performed the piece without a conductor.  一支小型合奏团在无人指挥的情况下演奏了这首曲子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>clamor</b></td><td>/ˈklæmər/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>喧闹，喧哗，噪声</td><td>a clamor of voices<br>一片人声喧哗</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A clamor of voices rose from the crowded courtyard.  拥挤的院子里传出一片人声喧哗。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 blare</td><td>/ˈbler/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>响亮刺耳的声音；轰响</td><td>the blare of trumpets<br>响亮刺耳的号声</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Music blared from the open windows of the passing car.  音乐从驶过汽车敞开的车窗里轰响出来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bluster</td><td>/ˈblʌstər/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>狂妄自大地大声说；喧闹的状态</td><td>bluster and threaten<br>大声吓唬威胁</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The manager began to bluster and threaten when questioned closely.  被仔细追问时，经理开始狂妄自大地大声吓唬威胁。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cacophony</td><td>/kəˈkɑːfəni/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>刺耳的声音</td><td>a cacophony of horns<br>刺耳的喇叭声</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A cacophony of horns filled the street during rush hour.  高峰时段街上充满刺耳的喇叭声。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 clangor</del></td><td>/ˈklæŋɡər/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>持续的巨响，铿锵声</td><td>the clangor of bells<br>钟声的铿锵</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The clangor of hammers filled the shipyard all morning.  锤子的巨响整个上午充满了船坞。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 discordance</td><td>/dɪsˈkɔːrdəns/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>不一致，不和</td><td>discordance between reports<br>各报告之间的矛盾</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 There is a clear discordance between the two witnesses' accounts.  两位证人的叙述之间存在明显不一致。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 howl</td><td>/ˈhaʊl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>长嚎，怒吼</td><td>howl at the moon<br>对月长嚎</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Wolves howled somewhere beyond the frozen ridge that night.  那晚狼在冰封山脊外某处长嚎。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 racket</td><td>/ˈrækɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>喧闹吵嚷声；球拍</td><td>make a terrible racket<br>发出可怕的喧闹</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The children upstairs made a racket all afternoon long.  楼上的孩子整个下午都在吵闹。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rattle</td><td>/ˈrætəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>发出咔嗒声；使慌乱</td><td>rattle the windows<br>把窗户震得作响</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The passing train rattled every window in the old house.  驶过的火车震得老屋的每扇窗都咔咔作响。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 roar</td><td>/ˈrɔːr/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>咆哮，怒吼；轰鸣</td><td>roar with anger<br>愤怒地咆哮</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The coach roared at the players from the sideline.  教练在场边对球员们大声咆哮。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tumult</td><td>/ˈtuːmʌlt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>骚动，暴动</td><td>the tumult of the crowd<br>人群的骚动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The tumult in the square grew louder as more protesters arrived.  随着更多抗议者到来，广场上的骚动越来越大。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 uproar</td><td>/ˈʌˌprɔːr/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>喧嚣，骚动</td><td>cause an uproar<br>引起轩然大波</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new tax plan caused an uproar among small business owners.  新税收方案在小企业主中引起轩然大波。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 vociferation</del></td><td>/voʊˌsɪfəˈreɪʃən/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>大声喊叫，喧闹</td><td>loud vociferation outside<br>外面的大声喧哗</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The debate ended in angry vociferation from both sides.  辩论以双方愤怒的大声叫嚷收场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 quiet</td><td>/ˈkwaɪət/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>安静的；平静的</td><td>keep the room quiet<br>保持房间安静</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The library stays quiet even during exam week.  即使在考试周，图书馆也保持安静。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 silence</td><td>/ˈsaɪləns/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>寂静，沉默；使沉默</td><td>break the silence<br>打破沉默</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A long silence followed his surprising announcement to the class.  他向全班宣布这惊人消息后是长久的沉默。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 still</td><td>/ˈstɪl/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>寂静无声的；寂静</td><td>the still of night<br>夜晚的寂静</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The air was still and the lake looked like glass.  空气静止无风，湖面像玻璃一样。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 stillness</td><td>/ˈstɪlnəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>寂静，静止</td><td>the stillness of dawn<br>黎明时的寂静</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Only a distant bell broke the stillness of the valley.  只有远处的钟声打破了山谷的寂静。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>clan</b></td><td>/klæn/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>（有共同爱好的）团体，帮派</td><td>a tight-knit clan<br>关系紧密的团体</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The whole gardening clan meets here on the first Sunday.  整个园艺团体每月第一个周日在这里聚会。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 body</td><td>/ˈbɑːdi/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>体现，具体表现；身体</td><td>body forth an idea<br>体现某种思想</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The statue seems to body forth every ideal the founders cherished.  这座雕像仿佛体现了创建者珍视的每一个理想。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bunch</td><td>/ˈbʌntʃ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>一群，一束，一串</td><td>a bunch of keys<br>一串钥匙</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A bunch of students waited outside the lecture hall.  一群学生在讲堂外等着。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 circle</td><td>/ˈsɜːrkəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>圈子，团体</td><td>a close circle of friends<br>亲密的朋友圈</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She belongs to a small circle of literary critics.  她属于一个小小的文学批评家圈子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 clique</td><td>/ˈkliːk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>派系，小团体</td><td>a tight clique<br>紧密的小团体</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A small clique controlled every decision in the department.  一个小团体控制着系里的每项决定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 community</td><td>/kəmˈjuːnəti/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>群体，共同体；社区</td><td>the local community<br>当地社区</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The research community reacted quickly to the surprising new findings.  学术界对这些惊人的新发现反应迅速。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 coterie</td><td>/ˈkoʊtəri/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>小圈子，密友团</td><td>a coterie of admirers<br>一小群仰慕者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The poet was surrounded by a coterie of loyal followers.  这位诗人身边围着一小圈忠实的追随者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 coven</td><td>/ˈkʌvən/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>女巫团；秘密小团体</td><td>a coven of witches<br>一群女巫</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Local legend claims a coven once met in these dark woods.  当地传说称曾有女巫团在这片幽暗的树林聚会。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 crowd</td><td>/ˈkraʊd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>一伙人，圈子</td><td>hang out with that crowd<br>和那伙人来往</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He started skipping class after joining the wrong crowd.  他结交了不良的一伙人后开始逃课。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fold</td><td>/ˈfoʊld/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>折叠；褶痕</td><td>fold the laundry<br>叠洗好的衣服</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She folded the letter twice and slipped it into her pocket.  她把信折了两次，塞进口袋。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 network</td><td>/ˈnetˌwɜːrk/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>人际网络，关系网</td><td>a professional network<br>职业人脉网</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She built a wide network of contacts in the publishing world.  她在出版界建立了广泛的人脉网。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pack</td><td>/ˈpæk/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>搬运，扛运；打包</td><td>pack supplies up the trail<br>把物资背上山路</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The guides packed our heavy gear up the steep trail.  向导把我们沉重的装备扛上陡峭的小路。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ring</td><td>/ˈrɪŋ/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>团伙，帮派</td><td>a smuggling ring<br>走私团伙</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Police finally broke up the counterfeit ring last month.  警方上个月终于捣毁了这个假币团伙。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 set</td><td>/ˈset/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词、形容词</td><td>使朝向，安置；设定</td><td>set a new course<br>设定新航向</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The captain set the ship's course toward the northern islands.  船长把船的航向设定为朝北方群岛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>cleave</b></td><td>/kliːv/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>劈开，割裂；紧贴，坚持</td><td>cleave the log in two<br>把木头劈成两半</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 One blow of the axe cleaved the log in two.  斧头一击就把木头劈成了两半。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 adhere</td><td>/ədˈhɪr/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>服从，遵守</td><td>adhere to the rules<br>遵守规则</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 All staff must adhere to the safety procedures without exception.  全体员工必须无例外地遵守安全规程。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cling</td><td>/klɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>紧贴；坚守（信念）</td><td>cling to tradition<br>坚守传统</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Older residents still cling to tradition despite all the changes.  尽管变化很多，年长的居民仍然坚守传统。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hew</td><td>/hjuː/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>砍；劈；坚守（原则）</td><td>hew to the line<br>严守准则</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new mayor promised to hew closely to the party line.  新市长承诺严格遵循党的路线。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stick</td><td>/ˈstɪk/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>坚持，不改口；粘住</td><td>stick to his story<br>坚持他的说法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Under hours of questioning the witness stuck to her original account.  在数小时的盘问下，证人仍坚持她最初的说法。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cut</td><td>/ˈkʌt/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>缩短；削减</td><td>cut the speech short<br>缩短讲话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The director cut twenty minutes from the film before its release.  导演在影片上映前剪掉了二十分钟。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 decouple</td><td>/diːˈkʌpəl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>分离，使脱钩</td><td>decouple growth from emissions<br>使增长与排放脱钩</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Economists hope to decouple energy use from economic growth.  经济学家希望使能源消耗与经济增长脱钩。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 disconnect</td><td>/dɪskəˈnekt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>断开，使分离</td><td>disconnect the power<br>切断电源</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Please disconnect the printer before you open its back panel.  打开后盖前请先断开打印机的连接。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 disjoin</del></td><td>/dɪsˈdʒɔɪn/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>使分开，使分离</td><td>disjoin the two parts<br>把两部分分开</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The engineer had to disjoin the pipes to replace a valve.  工程师得把管子分开才能更换阀门。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 dissever</del></td><td>/dɪˈsevər/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>分开，切断</td><td>dissever the two halves<br>把两半分开</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The war dissevered families that had lived together for generations.  战争把世代同住的家庭分割开来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dissociate</td><td>/dɪˈsoʊsieɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>分离，使分开</td><td>dissociate himself from it<br>与之划清界限</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The senator moved quickly to dissociate himself from the scandal.  这位参议员迅速与丑闻划清界限。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 divide</td><td>/dɪˈvaɪd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>分开，划分；分歧</td><td>divide into groups<br>分成小组</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The teacher divided the class into four groups of five.  老师把班级分成四个五人小组。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 part</td><td>/ˈpɑːrt/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>分开，分离</td><td>part the heavy curtains<br>分开厚重的窗帘</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She parted the heavy curtains and let the morning light in.  她分开厚重的窗帘，让晨光照进来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 ramify</del></td><td>/ˈræməˌfaɪ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>分叉，分成支系</td><td>ramify into many branches<br>分成许多分支</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The main artery ramifies into dozens of smaller vessels.  主动脉分叉成数十条更细的血管。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sever</td><td>/ˈsevər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>切断，断绝</td><td>sever all ties<br>断绝一切关系</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company severed all ties with its former supplier.  公司与前供应商断绝了一切关系。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 slice</td><td>/ˈslaɪs/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>切开，切成片</td><td>slice the bread thinly<br>把面包切成薄片</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He sliced the melon into eight neat pieces.  他把瓜切成了八块整齐的片。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 split</td><td>/ˈsplɪt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>分裂，分开</td><td>split into groups<br>分成小组</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The committee split into two camps over the proposal.  委员会因该提案分裂成两派。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sunder</td><td>/ˈsʌndər/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>分开，割断</td><td>sunder the alliance<br>使联盟破裂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The bitter dispute sundered an alliance that had lasted decades.  这场激烈的争端割断了维持数十年的联盟。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 defect</td><td>/ˈdiːfekt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、不及物动词</td><td>缺陷，瑕疵；叛逃</td><td>a manufacturing defect<br>制造缺陷</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A tiny defect in the wiring caused the entire system failure.  线路上一个微小缺陷导致了整个系统故障。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 join</td><td>/ˈdʒɔɪn/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>连接，接合；加入</td><td>join the two pieces<br>把两块接起来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Join the ends of the rope with a firm knot.  用牢固的结把绳子两端接起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 link</td><td>/ˈlɪŋk/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>联系，纽带；连接</td><td>a close link<br>紧密的联系</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Researchers found a close link between sleep and memory.  研究者发现睡眠与记忆之间存在紧密联系。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unify</td><td>/ˈjuːnəˌfaɪ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>统一，使成一体</td><td>unify the country<br>统一国家</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A common language helped unify the scattered tribes into one nation.  共同的语言帮助把分散的部落统一为一个民族。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unite</td><td>/juˈnaɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>联合，团结</td><td>unite against a threat<br>联合抵御威胁</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The crisis united the villagers as nothing else had.  这场危机以前所未有的方式团结了村民。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>cling</b></td><td>/klɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>紧贴；坚守（信念）</td><td>cling to tradition<br>坚守传统</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Older residents still cling to tradition despite all the changes.  尽管变化很多，年长的居民仍然坚守传统。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 adhere</td><td>/ədˈhɪr/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>服从，遵守</td><td>adhere to the rules<br>遵守规则</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 All staff must adhere to the safety procedures without exception.  全体员工必须无例外地遵守安全规程。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cleave</td><td>/kliːv/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>劈开，割裂；紧贴，坚持</td><td>cleave the log in two<br>把木头劈成两半</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 One blow of the axe cleaved the log in two.  斧头一击就把木头劈成了两半。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hew</td><td>/hjuː/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>砍；劈；坚守（原则）</td><td>hew to the line<br>严守准则</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new mayor promised to hew closely to the party line.  新市长承诺严格遵循党的路线。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stick</td><td>/ˈstɪk/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>坚持，不改口；粘住</td><td>stick to his story<br>坚持他的说法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Under hours of questioning the witness stuck to her original account.  在数小时的盘问下，证人仍坚持她最初的说法。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 defect</td><td>/ˈdiːfekt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、不及物动词</td><td>缺陷，瑕疵；叛逃</td><td>a manufacturing defect<br>制造缺陷</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A tiny defect in the wiring caused the entire system failure.  线路上一个微小缺陷导致了整个系统故障。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>coarse</b></td><td>/kɔːrs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>粗糙的，表面不平整的；粗俗的</td><td>coarse language<br>粗俗的言语</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The workers used coarse language that shocked the visiting students.  工人们说的粗话让来访的学生们大吃一惊。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 broken</td><td>/ˈbroʊkən/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>高低不平的；破损的</td><td>broken ground<br>高低不平的地面</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The horses slowed across the broken ground near the ravine.  马匹在峡谷附近高低不平的地面上放慢了脚步。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bumpy</td><td>/ˈbʌmpi/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>坑洼不平的</td><td>a bumpy road<br>坑洼的道路</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The bumpy track shook every bolt loose on the old truck.  坑洼的土路把老卡车上每颗螺栓都震松了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 irregular</td><td>/ɪˈreɡjələr/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不规则的，不平整的</td><td>an irregular shape<br>不规则的形状</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The path was made of irregular stones of many sizes.  这条小路由各种大小不规则的石头铺成。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 jagged</td><td>/ˈdʒæɡəd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>锯齿状的，参差不齐的</td><td>jagged rocks<br>锯齿状的岩石</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Jagged rocks below the cliff made landing impossible.  崖下锯齿状的岩石使登陆无法进行。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lumpy</td><td>/ˈlʌmpi/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>多疙块的，不平整的</td><td>a lumpy mattress<br>凹凸不平的床垫</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 I slept badly on a lumpy mattress in the attic room.  我在阁楼那张凹凸不平的床垫上没睡好。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pebbly</td><td>/ˈpɛbli/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>多卵石的，粗粒的</td><td>a pebbly beach<br>卵石遍布的海滩</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The pebbly beach hurt our feet but the water was clear.  卵石海滩硌脚，但水很清。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ragged</td><td>/ˈræɡɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>凹凸不平的，不光滑的</td><td>a ragged edge<br>参差不齐的边缘</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The saw left a ragged edge along the whole length of the board.  锯子在整块木板上留下参差不平的边缘。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rough</td><td>/ˈrʌf/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>粗糙的，粗暴的；艰苦的</td><td>a rough surface<br>粗糙的表面</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The road grew rough as we climbed into the hills.  我们爬上山时路变得坎坷不平。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 roughened</td><td>/ˈrʌfənd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>变粗糙的</td><td>roughened hands<br>变粗糙的双手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Years of rope work left his palms roughened and hard.  多年拉绳的活计让他手掌变得粗糙坚硬。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rugged</td><td>/ˈrʌɡəd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>坚毅的；粗糙不平的</td><td>rugged terrain<br>险峻的地形</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Only rugged travelers attempt that trail in the winter.  只有意志坚毅的旅行者才会在冬天走那条小径。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 scraggy</td><td>/ˈskræɡi/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>粗糙不平的；瘦骨嶙峋的</td><td>scraggy cliffs<br>嶙峋的峭壁</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Goats climbed the scraggy slope without any trouble.  山羊毫不费力地爬上那道嶙峋的坡。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 crass</td><td>/kræs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>粗俗的，愚钝的</td><td>crass commercialism<br>赤裸裸的商业气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His crass joke silenced the entire table at dinner.  他粗俗的玩笑让整桌人都沉默了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 crude</td><td>/ˈkruːd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>粗糙的，未加工的；粗俗的</td><td>crude oil<br>原油</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The furniture was crude but surprisingly sturdy and very cheap.  这家具做得粗糙，却意外结实又便宜。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 gross</td><td>/ˈɡroʊs/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>过于肥胖的；粗俗的</td><td>a gross, bloated figure<br>臃肿肥胖的身形</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The caricature gave the mayor a gross, swollen belly.  那幅漫画给市长画了个臃肿肥大的肚子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 incult</del></td><td>/ɪnˈkʌlt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>粗野的，未开化的</td><td>incult manners<br>粗野的举止</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The travelers found the mountain villages poor and their manners incult.  旅行者发现山村贫穷，村民举止粗野。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 lowbred</del></td><td>/ˈloʊˌbrɛd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>粗俗的，没教养的</td><td>lowbred manners<br>没教养的举止</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The hostess found his loud jokes lowbred and tiresome.  女主人觉得他大声的玩笑粗俗又乏味。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 raffish</td><td>/ˈræfɪʃ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>低俗的；放浪不羁的</td><td>a raffish charm<br>低俗却迷人的味道</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old actor had a raffish charm that everyone found irresistible.  这位老演员有种放浪不羁的魅力，人人难以抗拒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rude</td><td>/ˈruːd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>粗鲁的，无礼的</td><td>a rude remark<br>无礼的话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It was rude of him to leave without thanking the host.  他不谢主人就离开，很没礼貌。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 uncouth</td><td>/ʌnˈkuːθ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>粗鲁的，无教养的</td><td>uncouth table manners<br>粗野的餐桌举止</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His uncouth remarks at dinner offended nearly everyone at the table.  他在晚餐上粗鲁的言辞几乎惹恼了同桌所有人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 uncultivated</td><td>/ʌnˈkʌltɪveɪtɪd/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>未开垦的；未受教养的</td><td>uncultivated soil<br>未开垦的土壤</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Much of the island remains uncultivated and covered in scrub.  岛上大部分地方仍未开垦，长满灌木。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unpolished</td><td>/ʌnˈpɑlɪʃt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>粗糙的，不文雅的</td><td>unpolished manners<br>不文雅的举止</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His writing is unpolished but honest and often very funny.  他的文字粗糙，却真诚而常常很好笑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unrefined</td><td>/ˌʌnrɪˈfaɪnd/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>粗俗的，未提炼的</td><td>unrefined manners<br>粗俗的举止</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His unrefined manners embarrassed the whole table.  他粗俗的举止让整桌人尴尬。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 vulgar</td><td>/ˈvʌlɡər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>粗俗的，无教养的</td><td>vulgar display of wealth<br>粗俗的炫富</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their vulgar display of wealth embarrassed even their closest friends.  他们粗俗的炫富让连最亲近的朋友都感到难堪。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 even</td><td>/ˈiːvən/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>平坦的，均匀的</td><td>an even surface<br>平坦的表面</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Sand the board until the surface feels perfectly even.  把板子打磨到表面完全平整。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 flat</td><td>/ˈflæt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>单调无光的，乏味的</td><td>a flat performance<br>平淡无味的表演</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The paint dried to a flat, lifeless gray.  油漆干成了一种无光而死板的灰色。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 level</td><td>/ˈlevəl/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>水平的，平的</td><td>on level ground<br>在平地上</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Pitch the tent on level ground away from the stream.  把帐篷搭在远离溪水的平地上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 plane</td><td>/ˈpleɪn/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>平坦的；平面</td><td>a plane surface<br>平整的表面</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Grinding produced a perfectly plane surface for the lens.  研磨为这枚镜片做出了完全平整的表面。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 civilized</td><td>/ˈsɪvəˌlaɪzd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>文明的，有教养的</td><td>civilized behavior<br>文明的举止</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They settled the dispute in a calm, civilized manner over tea.  他们喝茶时以平静文明的方式解决了争端。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 cultivated</td><td>/ˈkʌltɪveɪtɪd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有教养的，有修养的</td><td>a cultivated taste in art<br>高雅的艺术品位</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her cultivated manners impressed everyone at the dinner.  她优雅的举止让晚宴上的每个人都印象深刻。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 cultured</td><td>/ˈkʌltʃərd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有教养的，有修养的</td><td>a cultured gentleman<br>有教养的绅士</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was a cultured man who read poetry in four languages.  他是个有教养的人，能读四种语言的诗。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 genteel</td><td>/dʒenˈtiːl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有教养的，不粗俗的</td><td>genteel manners<br>有教养的举止</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her genteel accent betrayed an expensive and very traditional upbringing.  她文雅的口音透露出昂贵而传统的成长背景。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 polished</td><td>/ˈpɑːlɪʃt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有教养的</td><td>polished manners<br>优雅得体的举止</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His polished manners impressed everyone at the formal dinner.  他优雅得体的举止让正式晚宴上的每个人都印象深刻。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 refined</td><td>/rəˈfaɪnd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>精炼的，纯净的；有教养的</td><td>refined sugar<br>精制糖</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Refined sugar has been stripped of every impurity.  精制糖已去除了所有杂质。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 smooth</td><td>/ˈsmuːð/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词、动词</td><td>温和的，圆滑的；平滑的</td><td>a smooth talker<br>说话圆滑的人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He is a smooth talker who never offends anyone in negotiations.  他谈判时言辞圆滑，从不得罪任何人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 tasteful</td><td>/ˈteɪstfəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有品味的，雅致的</td><td>tasteful interior decoration<br>雅致的室内装饰</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The whole room was furnished in a quiet, tasteful style.  整个房间以一种素净雅致的风格布置。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>cohesive</b></td><td>/koʊˈhiːsɪv/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有粘性的；有凝聚力的</td><td>a cohesive team<br>有凝聚力的团队</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two seasons together turned the players into a cohesive team.  两个赛季的磨合让球员成了一支有凝聚力的团队。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 adherent</td><td>/ədˈhɪrənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>追随者，拥护者</td><td>a devoted adherent<br>忠实的拥护者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The movement attracted thousands of adherents in a single year.  这场运动一年内就吸引了数千名拥护者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 adhesive</td><td>/ædˈhiːsɪv/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>粘性的；粘合剂</td><td>adhesive tape<br>胶带</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She sealed the heavy box with strong adhesive tape.  她用结实的胶带把重箱子封好。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 clingy</td><td>/ˈklɪŋi/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>黏的，黏附的</td><td>clingy wet sand<br>黏黏的湿沙</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The clingy mud held our boots at every step.  黏稠的泥每走一步都拽住我们的靴子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 gluey</td><td>/ˈɡluːi/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>胶状的，粘的</td><td>a gluey substance<br>胶状的物质</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old pot was coated with a gluey brown substance.  旧锅里裹着一层棕色的胶状物质。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 glutinous</td><td>/ˈɡluːtənəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>胶状的，粘的</td><td>glutinous rice<br>糯米</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The glutinous rice stuck to my fingers while I ate.  我吃的时候糯米粘在了手指上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 gummy</td><td>/ˈɡʌmi/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>黏性的，胶状的</td><td>a gummy substance<br>黏稠的物质</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The candy left a gummy residue on my fingers.  糖在我手指上留下了黏黏的残渣。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tacky</td><td>/ˈtæki/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>发黏的，未干的</td><td>still tacky paint<br>仍发黏的油漆</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Do not touch the varnish while it is still tacky.  清漆还发黏时不要碰。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tenacious</td><td>/təˈneɪʃəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>黏性强的；不屈不挠的</td><td>a tenacious opponent<br>顽强的对手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She proved a tenacious opponent who never conceded a single point.  她是个顽强的对手，从不让出一分。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 viscid</td><td>/ˈvɪsɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有粘性的</td><td>a viscid liquid<br>粘稠的液体</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A viscid sap oozed slowly from the cut in the bark.  一种粘性的树液从树皮的切口缓缓渗出。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 non-adhesive</td><td>/ˌnɑn ədˈhiːsɪv/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无黏性的，不粘的</td><td>a non-adhesive coating<br>无黏性涂层</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The pan has a non-adhesive lining that makes cleaning easy.  这个锅有不粘内衬，清洗很方便。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 non-viscous</td><td>/ˌnɑn ˈvɪskəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>非黏稠的，稀薄的</td><td>a non-viscous liquid<br>非黏稠的液体</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Water is a non-viscous fluid compared with thick honey.  与浓稠的蜂蜜相比，水是一种非黏稠液体。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 10

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>commiserate</b></td><td>/kəˈmɪzəreɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>表示怜悯，同情</td><td>commiserate with the losers<br>同情落败者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We stayed after the game to commiserate with the losing team.  比赛后我们留下来对落败的队伍表示同情。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ache</td><td>/ˈeɪk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>渴望；疼痛</td><td>ache for home<br>渴望回家</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After three years abroad she ached for her hometown.  在国外三年后，她十分渴望回到故乡。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bleed</td><td>/ˈbliːd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>流血；渗出，渗色</td><td>bleed through the bandage<br>渗过绷带</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The red dye bled onto the white shirt in the wash.  洗涤时红色染料渗到了白衬衫上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 compassionate</td><td>/kəmˈpæʃənət/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有同情心的，慈悲的</td><td>compassionate care<br>充满关怀的照护</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A compassionate nurse sat with the frightened child all night.  一位有同情心的护士整夜陪着那个受惊的孩子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 condole</td><td>/kənˈdoʊl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>表达同情</td><td>condole with the widow<br>向寡妇表示慰问</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Neighbors came all week to condole with the grieving widow.  邻居们整周都来慰问那位悲伤的寡妇。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sympathize</td><td>/ˈsɪmpəˌθaɪz/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>同情，表示怜悯</td><td>sympathize with her loss<br>同情她的丧亲之痛</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 I sympathize with anyone who has lost a job this year.  我同情今年失业的每一个人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 yearn</td><td>/ˈjɜːrn/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>渴望，向往</td><td>yearn for freedom<br>渴望自由</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After years abroad he yearned for the food of his childhood.  在国外多年后，他渴望儿时的食物。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>compatible</b></td><td>/kəmˈpætəbl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>一致的，能共存的</td><td>compatible with the system<br>与该系统兼容</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new driver is compatible with both older printers.  新驱动与两款旧打印机都兼容。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 accordant</del></td><td>/əˈkɔrdənt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>一致的，符合的</td><td>accordant with the law<br>与法律相符</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The ruling was accordant with decisions made in earlier cases.  该裁决与早先案件的判决一致。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 coherent</td><td>/koʊˈhɪrənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>连贯的，条理清晰的</td><td>a coherent argument<br>条理清晰的论证</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was too tired to give a coherent account of the accident.  他太累了，无法条理清晰地讲述事故。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 concordant</td><td>/kənˈkɔrdənt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>一致的，和谐的</td><td>concordant testimony<br>一致的证词</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Both witnesses gave concordant accounts of that evening.  两位证人对那天晚上的叙述一致。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 conformable</td><td>/kənˈfɔrməbəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>顺从的；一致的</td><td>conformable to the rules<br>符合规定的</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The design must be conformable to the city's new safety code.  设计必须符合城市新的安全规范。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 congruent</td><td>/ˈkɑːŋɡruənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>和谐一致的；全等的</td><td>congruent with the facts<br>与事实相符</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His account is congruent with the facts we already know.  他的说法与我们已知的事实相符。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 congruous</td><td>/ˈkɑŋɡruəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>一致的，适合的，协调的</td><td>congruous with the setting<br>与环境协调</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The plain wooden benches were congruous with the simple chapel.  素木长凳与这座朴素的礼拜堂很协调。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 consonant</td><td>/ˈkɑːnsənənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>和谐一致的</td><td>consonant with tradition<br>与传统相符</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His modest lifestyle is consonant with the values he preaches.  他朴素的生活方式与他宣扬的价值观相符。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 harmonious</td><td>/hɑːrˈmoʊniəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>和谐的，融洽的；悦耳的</td><td>a harmonious relationship<br>和谐的关系</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two neighbors maintained a harmonious relationship for over thirty years.  这两位邻居三十多年来保持着融洽的关系。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 conflicting</td><td>/kənˈflɪktɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>冲突的，矛盾的</td><td>conflicting accounts<br>相互矛盾的说法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Witnesses gave conflicting accounts of the driver's speed.  证人对司机车速的说法相互矛盾。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 incompatible</td><td>/ˌɪnkəmˈpætəbəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不相容的，矛盾的</td><td>incompatible goals<br>互不相容的目标</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Rapid growth and strict conservation seemed incompatible goals here.  在这里快速发展与严格保护似乎是矛盾的目标。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 incongruous</td><td>/ɪnˈkɑːŋɡruəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不协调的，不相称的</td><td>an incongruous mixture<br>不协调的混合</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His loud tie looked incongruous with the somber gray suit.  他花哨的领带与深灰西装很不协调。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 inconsistent</td><td>/ˌɪnkənˈsɪstənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不一致的，矛盾的</td><td>inconsistent with the facts<br>与事实不符</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His testimony was inconsistent with the facts established by the police.  他的证词与警方查明的事实不符。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 inharmonious</del></td><td>/ˌɪnhɑːrˈmoʊniəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不和谐的，不协调的</td><td>inharmonious colors<br>不协调的颜色</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new wing looked inharmonious beside the old stone chapel.  新翼楼在古老石砌小教堂旁显得不协调。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>compelling</b></td><td>/kəmˈpelɪŋ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>极具说服力的</td><td>a compelling reason<br>令人信服的理由</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He gave a compelling reason for leaving the project early.  他给出了提前离开项目的极具说服力的理由。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 conclusive</td><td>/kənˈkluːsɪv/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>决定性的，无可置疑的</td><td>conclusive proof<br>决定性的证据</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The tests were not conclusive, so the doctors ordered more.  检测结果并不确定，医生便要求再做检查。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 convincing</td><td>/kənˈvɪnsɪŋ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>令人信服的，有说服力的</td><td>convincing evidence<br>有说服力的证据</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She gave a convincing explanation for the missing money.  她对丢失的钱给出了令人信服的解释。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 decisive</td><td>/dɪˈsaɪsɪv/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>决定性的；果断的</td><td>a decisive victory<br>决定性的胜利</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her testimony was decisive in the trial.  她的证词在审判中起了决定性作用。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 effective</td><td>/ɪˈfektɪv/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有效的，起作用的</td><td>an effective solution<br>有效的解决办法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Simple hand washing is an effective way to prevent illness.  简单洗手是预防疾病的有效方法。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 forceful</td><td>/ˈfɔːrsfəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>强有力的，有说服力的</td><td>a forceful argument<br>有力的论据</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She made a forceful case for reforming the outdated grading system.  她为改革过时的评分制度提出了有力的论证。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 persuasive</td><td>/pərˈsweɪsɪv/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有说服力的，令人信服的</td><td>a persuasive speaker<br>有说服力的演讲者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His persuasive letter changed the committee's mind overnight.  他那封有说服力的信一夜之间改变了委员会的想法。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 satisfying</td><td>/ˈsætɪsˌfaɪɪŋ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>令人满意的；有说服力的</td><td>a satisfying explanation<br>令人满意的解释</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 No one offered a satisfying answer to the reporter's question.  没人对记者的问题给出令人满意的答复。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 strong</td><td>/strɔːŋ/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>强壮的，坚固的</td><td>strong arms<br>强壮的臂膀</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The bridge is strong enough to carry heavy trucks.  这座桥足够坚固，能承载重型卡车。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 telling</td><td>/ˈtelɪŋ/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有效的，显著的</td><td>a telling detail<br>意味深长的细节</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 One telling detail in the letter revealed the writer's true motive.  信中一个意味深长的细节揭示了写信人的真实动机。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 feeble</td><td>/ˈfiːbl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>衰弱的</td><td>a feeble voice<br>微弱的声音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old man answered in a feeble voice from the bed.  老人从床上用微弱的声音回答。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 weak</td><td>/ˈwiːk/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>脆弱的，虚弱的</td><td>a weak structure<br>脆弱的结构</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The bridge has a weak structure and cannot bear trucks.  这座桥结构脆弱，无法承载卡车。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>complacent</b></td><td>/kəmˈpleɪsənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>自满的，自鸣得意的</td><td>grow complacent about safety<br>对安全变得自满</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The team grew complacent about safety after years without accidents.  多年没有事故后，团队对安全变得自满。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 assured</td><td>/əˈʃʊrd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>自信的；有保证的</td><td>an assured manner<br>自信的举止</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She gave an assured performance despite the huge audience.  尽管观众众多，她的表演依然从容自信。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 consequential</td><td>/ˌkɑːnsəˈkwenʃəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>重要的；随之而来的</td><td>a consequential ruling<br>一项重要的裁决</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 That quiet vote proved the most consequential act of the session.  那次不起眼的投票成了本届会议最重要的举动。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 egoistic</td><td>/ˌiːɡoʊˈɪstɪk/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>利己的，以自我为中心的</td><td>egoistic motives behind it<br>其背后的利己动机</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics detected egoistic motives behind his sudden generosity toward the museum.  批评者察觉到他突然对博物馆慷慨背后的利己动机。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 overweening</td><td>/ˌoʊvərˈwiːnɪŋ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>傲慢的，专横的</td><td>overweening pride<br>过度的骄傲</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His overweening confidence led him to ignore every warning from the engineers.  他过度的自信使他忽视了工程师的每一次警告。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pompous</td><td>/ˈpɑːmpəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>浮夸的；傲慢的</td><td>a pompous speech<br>浮夸的演讲</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The pompous speech bored everyone in the crowded town hall.  那场浮夸的演讲让拥挤的市政厅里每个人都厌烦。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 prideful</td><td>/ˈpraɪdfəl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>骄傲的，自负的</td><td>a prideful refusal<br>高傲的拒绝</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His prideful manner cost him several close friendships.  他自负的态度让他失去了几段亲密友谊。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 proud</td><td>/ˈpraʊd/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>自豪的；傲慢的</td><td>too proud to ask<br>自傲得不肯求人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was too proud to admit that he needed help.  他太自傲，不肯承认自己需要帮助。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 smug</td><td>/smʌɡ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>自大的，自鸣得意的</td><td>a smug expression<br>自鸣得意的表情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He wore a smug expression after winning the argument.  争论获胜后他一脸自鸣得意。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 vain</td><td>/ˈveɪn/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>徒劳的；自负的</td><td>in vain<br>徒劳地</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They searched all night in vain for the missing dog.  他们整夜寻找失踪的狗，却是徒劳。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 vainglorious</td><td>/veɪnˈɡlɔːriəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>自负的，虚荣的</td><td>vainglorious boasting<br>虚荣的自夸</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The general's vainglorious speech impressed nobody in the room.  将军自吹自擂的演讲没打动房里任何人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 egoless</del></td><td>/ˈiːɡoʊləs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无自我的，不自我中心的</td><td>an egoless leader<br>没有自我的领导者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Good teamwork requires an almost egoless attitude from everyone.  良好的团队合作要求每个人几乎都放下自我。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 humble</td><td>/ˈhʌmbl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>卑微的，地位低下的；谦逊的</td><td>humble origins<br>卑微的出身</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He rose from humble origins to lead the company.  他出身卑微，最终领导了这家公司。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 modest</td><td>/ˈmɑːdəst/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>谦虚的；不大的，适度的</td><td>modest about her success<br>对成功很谦虚</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He stayed modest about his award and thanked his team.  他对自己的奖项很谦虚，还感谢了团队。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 uncomplacent</del></td><td>/ˌʌnkəmˈpleɪsənt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不自满的，不安于现状的</td><td>an uncomplacent attitude<br>不自满的态度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her uncomplacent attitude kept the team improving every season.  她不自满的态度让团队每个赛季都在进步。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>complementary</b></td><td>/ˌkɑːmplɪˈmentri/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>互补的</td><td>complementary colors<br>互补色</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their skills are complementary, which makes them an unusually effective research team.  他们的技能互补，因此成了一支异常高效的研究团队。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 correlative</td><td>/kəˈrɛlətɪv/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>相互关联的</td><td>correlative rights and duties<br>相关的权利与义务</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Freedom and responsibility are correlative ideas in his philosophy.  在他的哲学中，自由与责任是相互关联的概念。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 interdependent</td><td>/ˌɪntərdɪˈpendənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>相互依赖的</td><td>interdependent global economies<br>相互依存的全球经济</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Modern economies are deeply interdependent and rarely fail alone.  现代经济高度相互依赖，很少单独崩溃。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 reciprocal</td><td>/rɪˈsɪprəkəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>相互的，互惠的</td><td>a reciprocal agreement<br>互惠协议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two countries signed a reciprocal trade agreement yesterday.  两国昨天签署了一项互惠贸易协定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 supplemental</td><td>/ˌsʌpləˈmentəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>补充的，附加的</td><td>supplemental income<br>补充性收入</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She took a night job for supplemental income during college.  上大学时她做夜班工作以补充收入。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 supplementary</td><td>/ˌsʌpləˈmentəri/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>补充的，额外的</td><td>supplementary information<br>补充信息</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The teacher handed out supplementary reading for the weekend.  老师发了周末的补充阅读材料。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 contradictory</td><td>/ˌkɑːntrəˈdɪktəri/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>相互矛盾的</td><td>contradictory evidence<br>相互矛盾的证据</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The witnesses gave contradictory accounts of the accident.  证人们对事故给出了相互矛盾的说法。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 incompatible</td><td>/ˌɪnkəmˈpætəbəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不相容的，矛盾的</td><td>incompatible goals<br>互不相容的目标</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Rapid growth and strict conservation seemed incompatible goals here.  在这里快速发展与严格保护似乎是矛盾的目标。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>complicate</b></td><td>/ˈkɑːmplɪkeɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使复杂化</td><td>complicate matters further<br>使事情更复杂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 New evidence complicated the investigation and delayed the final report.  新证据使调查复杂化，并推迟了最终报告。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 complex</td><td>/ˈkɑːmpleks/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>复杂的；综合体</td><td>a complex argument<br>复杂的论证</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The paper presents a complex argument about ancient trade routes.  这篇论文提出了关于古代商路的复杂论证。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 embarrass</td><td>/ɪmˈbærəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使尴尬</td><td>embarrass him in public<br>在公众面前使他尴尬</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He tried to embarrass her in public and only embarrassed himself.  他想在公众面前让她尴尬，结果只是让自己出丑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 entangle</td><td>/ɪnˈtæŋɡl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使缠住，使卷入；使复杂</td><td>entangle the negotiations<br>使谈判变复杂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 New objections entangled the negotiations and delayed the deal for weeks.  新的反对意见使谈判陷入纠缠，把交易推迟了数周。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ravel</td><td>/ˈrævl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>解开，松开；阐明</td><td>ravel out the thread<br>把线头解开</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She patiently began to ravel out the tangled thread with a needle.  她耐心地用针把缠住的线头解开。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 perplex</td><td>/pərˈpleks/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使困惑，使费解</td><td>perplex the researchers<br>使研究者困惑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The strange results perplexed the researchers for several long months.  奇怪的结果让研究者困惑了好几个月。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sophisticate</td><td>/səˈfɪstəˌkeɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>使复杂化；老于世故者</td><td>sophisticate the design further<br>把设计进一步复杂化</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Engineers sophisticated the model until nobody could explain it.  工程师把模型改得越来越复杂，最后没人能解释清楚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tangle</td><td>/ˈtæŋɡl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>纠缠，使…纠结</td><td>tangle the fishing line<br>把鱼线弄缠住</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The wind tangled the fishing line around the branches overhead.  风把鱼线缠在头顶的树枝上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 simplify</td><td>/ˈsɪmpləˌfaɪ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>简化，使简明</td><td>simplify the application form<br>简化申请表</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new software simplifies the way teachers record grades.  新软件简化了老师登记成绩的方式。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 streamline</td><td>/ˈstriːmlaɪn/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>精简，使效率提高</td><td>streamline the approval process<br>精简审批流程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The hospital streamlined admissions and cut waiting times in half.  医院精简了入院手续，把等候时间减半。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>comprehend</b></td><td>/ˌkɑːmprɪˈhend/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>理解，了解；包括，包含</td><td>comprehend the full report<br>理解整份报告</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Few readers can comprehend the full report without expert help.  很少有读者能在没有专家帮助时理解整份报告。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 appreciate</td><td>/əˈpriːʃieɪt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>增值，涨价；欣赏</td><td>appreciate in value<br>价值上涨</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old farmhouse has appreciated considerably over the last decade.  这座老农舍在过去十年里大幅增值。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 apprehend</td><td>/ˌæprɪˈhend/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>逮捕；理解</td><td>apprehend the suspect<br>逮捕嫌疑人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Police apprehended the thief two streets from the shop.  警方在离商店两条街的地方逮住了小偷。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 assimilate</td><td>/əˈsɪməˌleɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>把…比拟为；吸收，同化</td><td>assimilate one case to another<br>把一个案例比拟另一个</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics assimilate her early work to French realism.  批评家把她的早期作品比作法国现实主义。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 behold</td><td>/bɪˈhoʊld/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>看见，注视</td><td>behold the sunrise<br>凝望日出</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The travelers stopped to behold the valley below them.  旅人们停下来注视脚下的山谷。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 catch</td><td>/ˈkætʃ/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>染上（疾病）；抓住</td><td>catch a cold<br>感冒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 You will catch a cold if you go out with wet hair.  你要是湿着头发出门就会感冒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 cognize</del></td><td>/ˈkɑɡnaɪz/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>认知，认识到</td><td>cognize the difference<br>认识到差别</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Infants gradually cognize the difference between faces and objects.  婴儿逐渐认知面孔与物体之间的差别。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 conceive</td><td>/kənˈsiːv/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>构想，想出；理解</td><td>conceive a bold plan<br>构想大胆的计划</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 I cannot conceive why she would refuse such a generous offer.  我无法理解她为何会拒绝这么优厚的条件。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 discern</td><td>/dɪˈsɜːrn/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>识别，辨别差异</td><td>discern a pattern<br>辨别出一种规律</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Even experts could barely discern a pattern in the scattered rainfall data.  就连专家也几乎辨不出这些零散降雨数据中的规律。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 grasp</td><td>/ˈɡræsp/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>抓紧，握牢；理解</td><td>grasp the handle<br>抓住把手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She grasped the rope tightly and pulled herself up.  她紧抓绳子，把自己拉了上去。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 perceive</td><td>/pərˈsiːv/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>察觉，理解</td><td>perceive a subtle change<br>察觉细微的变化</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She perceived a subtle change in his tone during dinner.  晚餐时她察觉到他语气的微妙变化。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 recognize</td><td>/ˈrekəɡˌnaɪz/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>认出，辨识</td><td>recognize a familiar face<br>认出熟悉的面孔</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He recognized her familiar handwriting on the faded old envelope.  他在褪色的旧信封上认出她熟悉的字迹。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 savvy</td><td>/ˈsævi/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>老练，机智；有见识的，精明能干的</td><td>political savvy<br>政治上的老练</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her political savvy helped the bill pass with almost no opposition.  她政治上的老练使那项议案几乎无异议通过。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 see</td><td>/ˈsiː/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>理解，明白</td><td>see the point<br>明白要点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Now I see why you were so worried last night.  现在我明白你昨晚为什么那么担心了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 seize</td><td>/ˈsiːz/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>领会，抓住（要点）</td><td>seize the main point<br>抓住要点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Good students quickly seize the main point of a difficult lecture.  好学生能迅速抓住难懂讲座的要点。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sense</td><td>/ˈsens/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>意思，含义；感觉</td><td>in a broad sense<br>广义上讲</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 In a broad sense, every artist borrows from earlier traditions.  广义上讲，每位艺术家都借鉴前人的传统。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 understand</td><td>/ˌʌndərˈstænd/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>理解，懂得</td><td>understand the question<br>理解问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 I finally understand why the experiment kept failing.  我终于理解实验为何一直失败。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 carry</td><td>/ˈkæri/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>支撑，承载</td><td>carry the weight<br>承载重量</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Four stone pillars carry the whole weight of the roof.  四根石柱承载着整个屋顶的重量。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 contain</td><td>/kənˈteɪn/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>控制，抑制；包含</td><td>contain the outbreak<br>控制疫情爆发</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Doctors worked around the clock to contain the outbreak.  医生们昼夜不停地努力控制疫情。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 embrace</td><td>/ɪmˈbreɪs/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>乐于接受；拥抱</td><td>embrace change<br>乐于接受变化</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Young workers quickly embraced the new technology.  年轻员工迅速接受了这项新技术。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 encompass</td><td>/ɪnˈkʌmpəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>包含，涵盖；包围</td><td>encompass every major topic<br>涵盖所有主要话题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A single volume can hardly encompass every major topic in modern physics.  一卷书很难涵盖现代物理学的所有主要话题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 entail</td><td>/ɪnˈteɪl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>牵涉，导致</td><td>entail considerable risk<br>牵涉相当大的风险</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Rebuilding the bridge would entail considerable risk to the workers below.  重建这座桥会给下方的工人带来相当大的风险。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 involve</td><td>/ɪnˈvɑːlv/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>涉及，包含；使参与</td><td>involve considerable extra work<br>涉及大量额外工作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The plan involves moving three departments to another building.  这个计划涉及把三个部门迁到另一栋楼。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 number</td><td>/ˈnʌmbər/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>共计，包括在内</td><td>number among the best<br>位列最佳之中</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The society numbers three thousand members across the whole country.  该协会在全国共有三千名会员。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 subsume</td><td>/səbˈsum/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>包含，归入</td><td>subsume it under one heading<br>把它归入一个标题下</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The smaller agency was subsumed into the national ministry.  这家小机构被并入了国家部门。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 take in</td><td>/teɪk ˈɪn/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>欺骗；收留；领会</td><td>be taken in by<br>被…欺骗</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Don't be taken in by his charming manner and generous promises.  别被他迷人的举止和大方的承诺骗了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 misapprehend</del></td><td>/ˌmɪsæprɪˈhɛnd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>误解，误会</td><td>misapprehend the question<br>误解了这个问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She misapprehended his joke and left the room angry.  她误解了他的玩笑，生气地离开了房间。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 misconceive</del></td><td>/ˌmɪskənˈsiːv/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>误解，误会</td><td>misconceive the question<br>误解问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Many readers misconceive the author's argument about free will.  许多读者误解了作者关于自由意志的论证。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 misinterpret</td><td>/ˌmɪsɪnˈtɜːrprɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>误解，误读</td><td>misinterpret the data<br>误读数据</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Readers often misinterpret irony as sincere praise.  读者常把讽刺误解为真诚的赞美。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 misperceive</del></td><td>/ˌmɪspərˈsiːv/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>错误地理解，误认</td><td>misperceive the situation<br>对形势判断错误</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Investors misperceived the risk and lost a great deal.  投资者错判了风险，损失惨重。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 misunderstand</td><td>/ˌmɪsʌndərˈstænd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>误解，误会</td><td>misunderstand the instructions<br>误解说明</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Readers often misunderstand the ending of that famous novel.  读者常误解那部著名小说的结尾。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 exclude</td><td>/ɪkˈskluːd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>排除，不包括</td><td>exclude him from the list<br>把他从名单中排除</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The price excludes taxes and delivery to remote areas.  该价格不包括税费和偏远地区的运费。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 leave</td><td>/ˈliːv/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>遗漏，未包括</td><td>leave out a name<br>遗漏一个名字</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The editor decided to leave that whole chapter out.  编辑决定把那一整章都删掉不用。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 omit</td><td>/oʊˈmɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>省略，遗漏，忽略</td><td>omit the details<br>省略细节</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The summary omits the details that made his argument convincing.  摘要省略了使他论证有说服力的细节。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 preclude</td><td>/prɪˈkluːd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>预先阻止</td><td>preclude further discussion<br>阻止进一步讨论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A lack of funding does not preclude us from starting the project.  资金不足并不能阻止我们启动这个项目。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>concrete</b></td><td>/ˈkɑːŋkriːt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>具体的，实在的；明确的</td><td>concrete evidence<br>确凿的证据</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The detective needed concrete evidence rather than clever theories.  侦探需要确凿的证据，而不是巧妙的推论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 effective</td><td>/ɪˈfektɪv/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有效的，起作用的</td><td>an effective solution<br>有效的解决办法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Simple hand washing is an effective way to prevent illness.  简单洗手是预防疾病的有效方法。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 existent</td><td>/ɪɡˈzɪstənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>存在的，现存的</td><td>the existent records<br>现存的记录</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Only two existent copies of that pamphlet remain in the archive.  档案馆里只剩那本小册子的两份现存副本。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 factual</td><td>/ˈfæktʃuəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>真实的，基于事实的</td><td>a factual account<br>真实的叙述</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The report gives a factual account of the whole accident.  这份报告对整起事故做了真实的叙述。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 genuine</td><td>/ˈdʒenjəwən/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>真正的，真实的；真诚的</td><td>genuine leather<br>真皮</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Experts confirmed that the signature on the letter was genuine.  专家确认信上的签名是真的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 real</td><td>/ˈriːl/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>真实的，真正的</td><td>a real event<br>真实事件</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The film is based on a real event from 1943.  这部电影基于1943年的一件真实事件。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 true</td><td>/ˈtruː/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>真实的，真正的</td><td>a true story<br>一个真实的故事</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The film is based on a true story from wartime.  这部电影取材于战时的一个真实故事。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 very</td><td>/ˈveri/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词、副词</td><td>完全的，正是那个的</td><td>the very top<br>最顶端处</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They climbed to the very top of the ruined tower.  他们爬到了那座残塔的最顶端。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 de facto</td><td>/ˌdiː ˈfæktoʊ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词、副词</td><td>事实上的，实际上的</td><td>the de facto leader<br>事实上的领导人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She became the de facto head of the company after his illness.  他病后她成了公司实际上的负责人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 conjectural</td><td>/kənˈdʒɛktʃərəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>推测的，猜测的</td><td>purely conjectural conclusions<br>纯属推测的结论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His account of the ancient battle remains largely conjectural.  他对那场古代战役的说法在很大程度上仍属推测。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 hypothetical</td><td>/ˌhaɪpəˈθetɪkl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>假设的；假设情形</td><td>a purely hypothetical case<br>纯属假设的情形</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The professor posed a hypothetical to test how students weighed competing rights.  教授提出一个假设情形，考察学生如何权衡相互冲突的权利。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ideal</td><td>/aɪˈdiːl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>理想，典范；理想的</td><td>an ideal solution<br>理想的解决办法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She remains the ideal of quiet courage for many students.  她仍是许多学生心中沉静勇气的典范。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 platonic</td><td>/pləˈtɑːnɪk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>柏拉图式的，纯精神的；理论上的</td><td>a platonic friendship<br>纯精神的友谊</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their relationship stayed strictly platonic for over a decade.  他们的关系十多年来始终是纯精神的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 suppositional</del></td><td>/ˌsʌpəˈzɪʃənəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>假定的，推测的</td><td>a suppositional argument<br>假定性的论证</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Most of his reasoning rested on suppositional premises.  他的大部分推理都建立在假定的前提上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 theoretical</td><td>/ˌθiːəˈretɪkəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>理论的，理论上的</td><td>purely theoretical physics<br>纯理论物理</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His objection is theoretical and has no bearing on daily practice.  他的反对是理论性的，与日常实践无关。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>condemn</b></td><td>/kənˈdem/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>谴责（…为不道德的、邪恶的）</td><td>condemn the attack<br>谴责这次袭击</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 World leaders quickly condemned the attack on the hospital.  各国领导人迅速谴责了对医院的袭击。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 anathematize</del></td><td>/əˈnæθəmətaɪz/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>诅咒，痛斥</td><td>anathematize the heretics<br>诅咒异端者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The council met to anathematize anyone denying the doctrine.  会议召开是为了诅咒任何否认该教义的人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 censure</td><td>/ˈsenʃər/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>公开表示反对，谴责</td><td>a vote of censure<br>谴责性投票</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The council voted to censure the mayor for his conduct.  议会投票谴责市长的行为。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 damn</td><td>/dæm/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>（在道德上）谴责</td><td>damn the whole practice<br>谴责整套做法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics damned the novel as cruel and morally irresponsible.  批评者谴责这部小说残忍且在道德上不负责任。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 decry</td><td>/dɪˈkraɪ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>强烈反对，否定</td><td>decry the new policy<br>强烈反对新政策</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Local doctors decried the plan to close the only children's clinic.  当地医生强烈反对关闭唯一一家儿童诊所的计划。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 denounce</td><td>/dɪˈnaʊns/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>公开指责</td><td>denounce corruption<br>公开指责腐败</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Local leaders publicly denounced the corruption exposed by the newspaper.  当地领导人公开谴责报纸曝光的腐败行为。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 execrate</del></td><td>/ˈeksɪkreɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>痛恨，咒骂</td><td>execrate a tyrant<br>痛斥暴君</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Historians still execrate the tyrant for his cruelty.  史学家仍因其残暴而痛斥这个暴君。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 reprimand</td><td>/ˈreprəˌmænd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>训斥，正式谴责</td><td>reprimand an employee<br>训斥员工</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The officer was reprimanded for leaving his post early.  这名警官因提早离岗而受到训斥。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 reprehend</del></td><td>/reprɪˈhend/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>谴责，指责</td><td>reprehend such conduct<br>谴责这种行为</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The committee did not hesitate to reprehend the officer for his careless report.  委员会毫不犹豫地谴责了那名官员草率的报告。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 reproach</td><td>/rɪˈproʊtʃ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>批评，责备</td><td>reproach oneself for<br>因某事自责</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She reproached him for never returning her calls.  她责备他从不回她的电话。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 reprobate</td><td>/ˈreprəbeɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>堕落者，道德败坏的人</td><td>an old reprobate<br>一个老无赖</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The novel's hero is a charming reprobate with no money.  小说的主角是个迷人却身无分文的堕落者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 commend</td><td>/kəˈmend/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>赞扬，表扬</td><td>commend her courage<br>赞扬她的勇气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The principal commended her courage in front of the whole school.  校长在全校面前赞扬了她的勇气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 endorse</td><td>/ɪnˈdɔːrs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>公开支持</td><td>endorse a candidate<br>公开支持某候选人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The union voted to endorse the mayor for a second term.  工会投票支持市长连任。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 extol</td><td>/ɪkˈstoʊl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>赞美；吹捧</td><td>extol his virtues<br>赞美他的品德</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Neighbors still extol his kindness during the long winter flood.  邻居们至今仍赞美他在漫长冬季洪水期间的善举。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 laud</td><td>/ˈlɔːd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>赞美，称赞</td><td>laud his courage<br>赞扬他的勇气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics lauded the young pianist for her fearless interpretation.  评论家赞扬这位年轻钢琴家大胆的演绎。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 praise</td><td>/ˈpreɪz/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>赞扬，称赞</td><td>win high praise<br>获得高度赞扬</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her first novel won high praise from serious critics.  她的第一部小说赢得了严肃评论家的高度赞扬。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>conditional</b></td><td>/kənˈdɪʃənl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有条件的，受制约的</td><td>a conditional offer<br>有条件的录取通知</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She received a conditional offer from her favorite university.  她收到了心仪大学的有条件录取通知。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 contingent</td><td>/kənˈtɪndʒənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>视条件而定的；分遣队</td><td>contingent on approval<br>取决于批准</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The bonus is contingent on the company meeting its yearly targets.  奖金取决于公司是否达成年度目标。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dependent</td><td>/dɪˈpendənt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>依赖的，取决于…的</td><td>dependent on foreign aid<br>依赖外国援助</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The village remains dependent on rainfall for its entire harvest.  这个村子的全部收成仍然取决于降雨。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 subject</td><td>/ˈsʌbdʒekt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>取决于（其他因素）的，受制于</td><td>subject to approval<br>须经批准</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The entire plan is subject to approval by the city council.  整个方案须经市议会批准。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tentative</td><td>/ˈtentətɪv/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>暂时性的，试探性的</td><td>a tentative agreement<br>暂时性协议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two sides reached a tentative agreement late on Friday night.  双方在周五深夜达成暂时性协议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 absolute</td><td>/ˈæbsəluːt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>专制的；无限的；纯净不掺杂的；确凿的</td><td>absolute power<br>绝对的权力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The king held absolute power over his subjects.  国王对臣民拥有绝对权力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 categorical</td><td>/ˌkætəˈɡɔːrɪkl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>没有例外的；绝对的</td><td>a categorical denial<br>断然的否认</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The minister issued a categorical denial of the corruption charges.  部长断然否认了腐败指控。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unconditional</td><td>/ˌʌnkənˈdɪʃənəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无条件的，绝对的</td><td>unconditional surrender<br>无条件投降</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The treaty demanded the unconditional withdrawal of all foreign troops.  条约要求所有外国军队无条件撤离。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>
